   419→    if (!projectImageSettings) return;
   420→
   421→    if (projectImageSettings.generationSource) {
   422→      setGenerationSource(projectImageSettings.generationSource);
   423→    }
   424→    const textModel = projectImageSettings.selectedTextModel || 'qwen-image';
   425→    if (projectImageSettings.selectedTextModel) {
   426→      setSelectedTextModel(projectImageSettings.selectedTextModel);
   427→    }
   428→    // Store for LORA initialization (done in separate effect after loraManager is created)
   429→    initializedTextModelRef.current = textModel;
   430→    hasInitializedGenerationSource.current = true;
   431→  }, [projectImageSettings, selectedProjectId, isLoadingProjectSettings, setGenerationSource, setSelectedTextModel]);
   432→
   433→  // Initialize LORAs from per-category storage (runs after loraManager is created)
   434→  // Categories: 'qwen' (all Qwen models + by-reference) and 'z-image'
   435→  // NOTE: This effect must be AFTER useGenerationSource so generationSource/selectedTextModel are defined
   436→  const hasInitializedLoras = useRef(false);
   437→  useEffect(() => {
   438→    if (isLoadingProjectSettings) return;
   439→    if (hasInitializedLoras.current) return;
   440→
   441→    // Determine current category based on generation source and model
   442→    const textModel = initializedTextModelRef.current || selectedTextModel;
   443→    const currentSource = projectImageSettings?.generationSource || generationSource;
   444→    // by-reference always uses 'qwen' category
   445→    const category: LoraCategory = currentSource === 'by-reference' ? 'qwen' : getLoraCategoryForModel(textModel);
   446→
   447→    // Try new category-based storage first, fall back to old per-model storage for migration
   448→    let categoryLoras: ActiveLora[] = [];
   449→    if (projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByCategory) {
   450→      categoryLoras = projectImageSettings.selectedLorasByCategory[category] ?? [];
   451→    } else if (projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByTextModel) {
   452→      // Migration: use old per-model storage
   453→      categoryLoras = projectImageSettings.selectedLorasByTextModel[textModel] ?? [];
   454→    }
   455→
   456→    if (categoryLoras.length > 0) {
   457→      loraManager.setSelectedLoras(categoryLoras);
   458→    }
   459→    hasInitializedLoras.current = true;
   460→  }, [projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByCategory, projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByTextModel, projectImageSettings?.generationSource, isLoadingProjectSettings, loraManager, selectedTextModel, generationSource]);
   461→
   462→  // ============================================================================
   463→  // Prompt Management (extracted hook)
   464→  // ============================================================================
   465→  const {
   466→    prompts,
   467→    masterPromptText,
   468→    effectivePromptMode,
   469→    currentBeforePromptText,
   470→    currentAfterPromptText,
   471→    actionablePromptsCount,
   472→    lastKnownPromptCount,
   473→    setPrompts,
   474→    setMasterPromptText,
   475→    setEffectivePromptMode,
   476→    setCurrentBeforePromptText,
   477→    setCurrentAfterPromptText,
   478→    handleAddPrompt,
   479→    handleUpdatePrompt,
   480→    handleRemovePrompt,
   481→    handleDeleteAllPrompts,
   482→    handleSavePromptsFromModal,
   483→  } = usePromptManagement({
   484→    associatedShotId,
   485→    effectiveShotId,
   486→    shotPromptSettings: {
   487→      entityId: shotPromptSettings.entityId,
   488→      status: shotPromptSettings.status,
   489→      settings: shotPromptSettings.settings,
   490→      updateField: shotPromptSettings.updateField,
   491→    },
   492→    noShotPrompts,
   493→    setNoShotPrompts,
   494→    noShotMasterPrompt,
   495→    setNoShotMasterPrompt,
   496→    promptMode,
   497→    setPromptMode,
   498→    beforeEachPromptText,
   499→    setBeforeEachPromptText,
   500→    afterEachPromptText,
   501→    setAfterEachPromptText,
   502→    ready,
   503→    markAsInteracted,
   504→    generatePromptId,
   505→  });
   506→
   507→  // ============================================================================
   508→  // Reference Management (extracted hook)
   509→  // ============================================================================
   510→  const {
   511→    styleReferenceStrength,
   512→    subjectStrength,
   513→    subjectDescription,
   514→    inThisScene,
   515→    inThisSceneStrength,
   516→    referenceMode,
   517→    styleBoostTerms,
   518→    isUploadingStyleReference,
   519→    styleReferenceImageDisplay,
   520→    styleReferenceImageGeneration,
   521→    handleStyleReferenceUpload,
   522→    handleResourceSelect,
   523→    handleSelectReference,
   524→    handleDeleteReference,
   525→    handleUpdateReferenceName,
   526→    handleToggleVisibility,
   527→    handleRemoveStyleReference,
   528→    handleStyleStrengthChange,
   529→    handleSubjectStrengthChange,
   530→    handleSubjectDescriptionChange,
   531→    handleSubjectDescriptionFocus,
   532→    handleSubjectDescriptionBlur,
   533→    handleInThisSceneChange,
   534→    handleInThisSceneStrengthChange,
   535→    handleStyleBoostTermsChange,
   536→    handleReferenceModeChange,
   537→  } = useReferenceManagement({
   538→    selectedProjectId,
   539→    effectiveShotId,
   540→    selectedReferenceId,
   541→    selectedReferenceIdByShot,
   542→    referencePointers,
   543→    hydratedReferences,
   544→    selectedReference,

   554→    setHiresFixConfig,

   556→
   557→  // Computed values from useReferenceManagement (aliases for backwards compatibility)
   558→  const currentStyleStrength = styleReferenceStrength;
   559→  const currentSubjectStrength = subjectStrength;
   560→  const currentSubjectDescription = subjectDescription;
   561→  const effectiveSubjectDescription = currentSubjectDescription.trim() || 'this character';
   562→  const currentInThisScene = inThisScene;
   563→  const currentInThisSceneStrength = inThisSceneStrength;
   564→  const currentStyleBoostTerms = styleBoostTerms;
   565→
   566→  // ============================================================================
   567→  // Form Submission (extracted hook)
   568→  // ============================================================================
   569→  const {
   570→    handleSubmit,
   571→    handleGenerateAndQueue,
   572→    handleUseExistingPrompts,
   573→    handleNewPromptsLikeExisting,

   575→    selectedProjectId,

   580→    currentBeforePromptText,
   581→    currentAfterPromptText,
   582→    currentStyleBoostTerms,

   585→    effectivePromptMode,

   587→    actionablePromptsCount,

   589→    selectedTextModelRef,
   590→    styleReferenceImageGeneration,
   591→    currentStyleStrength,
   592→    currentSubjectStrength,
   593→    effectiveSubjectDescription,
   594→    currentInThisScene,
   595→    currentInThisSceneStrength,

   603→
   604→
   605→  // NOTE: currentBeforePromptText, setCurrentBeforePromptText, currentAfterPromptText,
   606→  //       setCurrentAfterPromptText now come from usePromptManagement hook
   607→  // NOTE: prompt count effects and localStorage shot settings now in usePromptManagement hook
   608→
   609→  // Consolidated debug logging (enable via VITE_DEBUG_IMAGE_GEN=true)
   610→  useEffect(() => {
   611→    if (!DEBUG_IMAGE_GEN) return;
   612→  }, [DEBUG_IMAGE_GEN, ready, isSaving, associatedShotId, selectedProjectId, prompts.length, shotPromptSettings.status]);
   613→
   614→  // Apply initialShotId once after hydration (takes precedence over persisted value)
   615→  // If initialShotId is explicitly null, reset to None (opened from outside shot context)
   616→  // IMPORTANT: Do NOT call markAsInteracted() here - initialShotId is a temporary context
   617→  // override (e.g., from modal opened in shot context), not a user preference to persist.
   618→  // The tool page's persisted shot selection should only change when explicitly changed by user.
   619→  const hasAppliedInitialShotId = useRef(false);
   620→  useEffect(() => {
   621→    // Only apply once, after hydration is complete
   622→    // Only override when initialShotId is a specific shot ID (truthy string)
   623→    // null or undefined = keep the persisted value from project settings
   624→    if (ready && !hasAppliedInitialShotId.current && shots) {
   625→      if (initialShotId) {
   626→        // initialShotId was provided as a specific shot - set to that shot if it exists
   627→        const shotExists = shots.some(shot => shot.id === initialShotId);
   628→        if (shotExists && associatedShotId !== initialShotId) {
   629→          setAssociatedShotId(initialShotId);
   630→          // Don't persist - this is a temporary context override
   631→        }
   632→      }
   633→      // If initialShotId is null/undefined, keep the persisted value from project settings
   634→      hasAppliedInitialShotId.current = true;
   635→    }
   636→  }, [ready, initialShotId, shots, associatedShotId]);
   637→
   638→  // Reset associatedShotId if the selected shot no longer exists (e.g., was deleted)
   639→  useEffect(() => {
   640→    if (associatedShotId && shots) {
   641→      const shotExists = shots.some(shot => shot.id === associatedShotId);
   642→      if (!shotExists) {
   643→        setAssociatedShotId(null);
   644→        markAsInteracted();
   645→      }
   646→    }
   647→  }, [associatedShotId, shots, markAsInteracted]);
   648→
   649→  // NOTE: initializedEntitiesRef and prompt initialization effect now in usePromptManagement hook
   650→  // NOTE: actionablePromptsCount now comes from usePromptManagement hook
   651→
   652→  useImperativeHandle(ref, () => ({
   653→    applySettings: (settings: DisplayableMetadata) => {
   654→      markAsInteracted();
   655→      // Apply settings to the current shot's prompts
   656→      setPrompts([{
   657→        id: generatePromptId(),
   658→        fullPrompt: settings.prompt || '',
   659→        shortPrompt: settings.shortPrompt
   660→      }]);
   661→      setImagesPerPrompt(1);
   662→
   663→      if (settings.activeLoras && settings.activeLoras.length > 0 && availableLoras.length > 0) {
   664→        const newSelectedLoras: ActiveLora[] = [];
   665→        settings.activeLoras.forEach(metaLora => {
   666→          const foundFullLora = availableLoras.find(al => al['Model ID'] === metaLora.id);
   667→          if (foundFullLora) {
   668→            newSelectedLoras.push({
   669→              id: metaLora.id,
   670→              name: metaLora.name,
   671→              path: metaLora.path,
   672→              strength: metaLora.strength,
   673→              previewImageUrl: foundFullLora.Images && foundFullLora.Images.length > 0 ? foundFullLora.Images[0].url : metaLora.previewImageUrl
   674→            });
   675→          }
   676→        });
   677→        loraManager.setSelectedLoras(newSelectedLoras);
   678→      } else {
   679→        loraManager.setSelectedLoras([]);
   680→      }
   681→      // Note: beforeEachPromptText/afterEachPromptText are NOT restored - they reset on page load
   682→    },
   683→    getAssociatedShotId: () => associatedShotId,
   684→  }));
   685→
   686→  // ============================================================================
   687→  // LORA Handlers (extracted hook)
   688→  // ============================================================================
   689→  const {
   690→    handleAddLora,
   691→    handleRemoveLora,
   692→    handleLoraStrengthChange,
   693→  } = useLoraHandlers({
   694→    loraManager: {
   695→      selectedLoras: loraManager.selectedLoras,
   696→      handleAddLora: loraManager.handleAddLora,
   697→      handleRemoveLora: loraManager.handleRemoveLora,
   698→      handleLoraStrengthChange: loraManager.handleLoraStrengthChange,
   699→      handleLoadProjectLoras: loraManager.handleLoadProjectLoras,
   700→    },
   701→    markAsInteracted,
   702→    generationSource,
   703→    selectedTextModel,
   704→    projectImageSettings,
   705→    updateProjectImageSettings,
   706→  });
   707→
   708→  // NOTE: Reference handlers (handleStyleReferenceUpload, handleResourceSelect, handleSelectReference,
   709→  //       handleDeleteReference, handleUpdateReference, handleUpdateReferenceName, handleToggleVisibility,
   710→  //       handleRemoveStyleReference, handleStyleStrengthChange, handleSubjectStrengthChange,
   711→  //       handleSubjectDescriptionChange, handleSubjectDescriptionFocus, handleSubjectDescriptionBlur,
   712→  //       handleInThisSceneChange, handleInThisSceneStrengthChange, handleStyleBoostTermsChange,
   713→  //       handleReferenceModeChange) now come from useReferenceManagement hook
   714→
   715→  // NOTE: Prompt handlers (handleAddPrompt, handleUpdatePrompt, handleRemovePrompt, handleDeleteAllPrompts,
   716→  //       handleSavePromptsFromModal) now come from usePromptManagement hook
   717→
   718→
   719→  // Handle creating a new shot
   720→  const handleCreateShot = useCallback(async (shotName: string, files: File[]) => {
   721→    // Use unified shot creation - handles inheritance, events, lastAffected automatically
   722→    const result = await createShot({
   723→      name: shotName,
   724→      files: files.length > 0 ? files : undefined,
   725→      dispatchSkeletonEvents: false, // No skeleton needed in form context
   726→      onSuccess: () => {
   727→        // Invalidate and refetch shots to update the list
   728→        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED](selectedProjectId!) });
   729→        queryClient.refetchQueries({ [REDACTED](selectedProjectId!) });
   730→      },
   731→    });
   732→
   733→    if (!result) {
   734→      // Error already shown by useShotCreation
   735→      return;
   736→    }
   737→
   738→    // Note: Settings inheritance is handled automatically by useShotCreation
   739→    
   740→    // Switch to the newly created shot
   741→    markAsInteracted();
   742→    setAssociatedShotId(result.shotId);
   743→    uiActions.setCreateShotModalOpen(false);
   744→  }, [createShot, markAsInteracted, queryClient, selectedProjectId, uiActions]);
   745→
   746→  // Optimize event handlers with useCallback to prevent recreating on each render
   747→  const handleSliderChange = useCallback((setter: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<number>>) => (value: number) => {
   748→    markAsInteracted();
   749→    setter(value);
   750→  }, [markAsInteracted]);
   751→
   752→  // Ensure the `promptIdCounter` is always ahead of any existing numeric IDs.
   753→  // This prevents duplicate IDs which caused multiple prompts to update together.
   754→  useEffect(() => {
   755→    let nextId = prompts.reduce((max, p) => {
   756→      const match = /^prompt-(\d+)$/.exec(p.id || "");
   757→      if (match) {
   758→        const num = parseInt(match[1], 10) + 1;
   759→        return num > max ? num : max;
   760→      }
   761→      return max;
   762→    }, 1);
   763→
   764→    // Resolve any duplicate IDs on the fly by assigning new ones.
   765→    const seen = new Set<string>();
   766→    let hadDuplicates = false;
   767→    const dedupedPrompts = prompts.map(prompt => {
   768→      if (!seen.has(prompt.id)) {
   769→        seen.add(prompt.id);
   770→        return prompt;
   771→      }
   772→      hadDuplicates = true;
   773→      // Duplicate found – give it a fresh ID.
   774→      const newId = `prompt-${nextId++}`;
   775→      seen.add(newId);
   776→      return { ...prompt, id: newId };
   777→    });
   778→
   779→    if (hadDuplicates) {
   780→      setPrompts(dedupedPrompts);
   781→    }
   782→
   783→    if (nextId > promptIdCounter.current) {
   784→      promptIdCounter.current = nextId;
   785→    }
   786→  }, [prompts, setPrompts]);
   787→
   788→  // Handle shot change with proper prompt initialization
   789→  const handleShotChange = (value: string) => {
   790→    markAsInteracted();
   791→    const newShotId = value === "none" ? null : value;
   792→
   793→    // usePersistentToolState auto-syncs associatedShotId to DB
   794→    setAssociatedShotId(newShotId);
   795→
   796→    // Call the parent callback if provided
   797→    if (onShotChange) {
   798→      onShotChange(newShotId);
   799→    }
   800→
   801→    // Note: Prompts for the new shot will be loaded automatically via useAutoSaveSettings
   802→    // and initialized if empty via the initialization effect
   803→    markAsInteracted();
   804→  };
   805→
   806→  // Note: Form fields are NOT automatically synced when selecting a reference
   807→  // This allows users to maintain their current settings when switching references
   808→
   809→  // Form fields maintain their current values independent of selected reference
   810→  // This allows users to keep their settings when switching references
   811→
   812→  // ============================================================================
   813→  // Context Value (for sections to pull from)
   814→  // ============================================================================
   815→
   816→  const coreState = useMemo<FormCoreState>(() => ({
   817→    selectedProjectId,
   818→    associatedShotId,
   819→    effectiveShotId,
   820→    isGenerating: automatedSubmitButton.isSubmitting,
   821→    ready,
   822→  }), [selectedProjectId, associatedShotId, effectiveShotId, automatedSubmitButton.isSubmitting, ready]);
   823→
   824→  const promptState = useMemo<FormPromptState>(() => ({
   825→    prompts,
   826→    masterPromptText,
   827→    effectivePromptMode,
   828→    actionablePromptsCount,
   829→    currentBeforePromptText,
   830→    currentAfterPromptText,
   831→    lastKnownPromptCount,
   832→  }), [prompts, masterPromptText, effectivePromptMode, actionablePromptsCount, currentBeforePromptText, currentAfterPromptText, lastKnownPromptCount]);
   833→
   834→  const promptHandlersValue = useMemo<FormPromptHandlers>(() => ({
   835→    setPrompts,
   836→    setMasterPromptText,
   837→    setEffectivePromptMode,
   838→    setCurrentBeforePromptText,
   839→    setCurrentAfterPromptText,
   840→    handleAddPrompt,
   841→    handleUpdatePrompt,
   842→    handleRemovePrompt,
   843→    handleDeleteAllPrompts,
   844→    markAsInteracted,
   845→  }), [setPrompts, setMasterPromptText, setEffectivePromptMode, setCurrentBeforePromptText, setCurrentAfterPromptText, handleAddPrompt, handleUpdatePrompt, handleRemovePrompt, handleDeleteAllPrompts, markAsInteracted]);
   846→
   847→  const referenceState = useMemo<FormReferenceState>(() => ({
   848→    references: hydratedReferences,
   849→    selectedReferenceId: displayedReferenceId,
   850→    referenceMode,
   851→    styleReferenceStrength,
   852→    subjectStrength,
   853→    subjectDescription,
   854→    inThisScene,
   855→    inThisSceneStrength,
   856→    styleBoostTerms,
   857→    isUploadingStyleReference,
   858→    styleReferenceImageDisplay,
   859→  }), [hydratedReferences, displayedReferenceId, referenceMode, styleReferenceStrength, subjectStrength, subjectDescription, inThisScene, inThisSceneStrength, styleBoostTerms, isUploadingStyleReference, styleReferenceImageDisplay]);
   860→
   861→  const referenceHandlersValue = useMemo<FormReferenceHandlers>(() => ({
   862→    onSelectReference: handleSelectReference,
   863→    onDeleteReference: handleDeleteReference,
   864→    onUpdateReferenceName: handleUpdateReferenceName,
   865→    onStyleUpload: handleStyleReferenceUpload,
   866→    onStyleRemove: handleRemoveStyleReference,
   867→    onStyleStrengthChange: handleStyleStrengthChange,
   868→    onSubjectStrengthChange: handleSubjectStrengthChange,
   869→    onSubjectDescriptionChange: handleSubjectDescriptionChange,
   870→    onSubjectDescriptionFocus: handleSubjectDescriptionFocus,
   871→    onSubjectDescriptionBlur: handleSubjectDescriptionBlur,
   872→    onInThisSceneChange: handleInThisSceneChange,
   873→    onInThisSceneStrengthChange: handleInThisSceneStrengthChange,
   874→    onReferenceModeChange: handleReferenceModeChange,
   875→    onStyleBoostTermsChange: handleStyleBoostTermsChange,
   876→    onToggleVisibility: handleToggleVisibility,
   877→    onResourceSelect: handleResourceSelect,
   878→  }), [handleSelectReference, handleDeleteReference, handleUpdateReferenceName, handleStyleReferenceUpload, handleRemoveStyleReference, handleStyleStrengthChange, handleSubjectStrengthChange, handleSubjectDescriptionChange, handleSubjectDescriptionFocus, handleSubjectDescriptionBlur, handleInThisSceneChange, handleInThisSceneStrengthChange, handleReferenceModeChange, handleStyleBoostTermsChange, handleToggleVisibility, handleResourceSelect]);
   879→
   880→  const loraState = useMemo<FormLoraState>(() => ({
   881→    selectedLoras: loraManager.selectedLoras,
   882→    availableLoras: availableLoras ?? [],
   883→  }), [loraManager.selectedLoras, availableLoras]);
   884→
   885→  const loraHandlersValue = useMemo<FormLoraHandlers>(() => ({
   886→    handleAddLora,
   887→    handleRemoveLora,
   888→    handleLoraStrengthChange,
   889→  }), [handleAddLora, handleRemoveLora, handleLoraStrengthChange]);
   890→
   891→  const contextValue = useContextValue({
   892→    uiState,
   893→    uiActions,
   894→    core: coreState,
   895→    prompts: promptState,
   896→    promptHandlers: promptHandlersValue,
   897→    references: referenceState,
   898→    referenceHandlers: referenceHandlersValue,
   899→    loras: loraState,
   900→    loraHandlers: loraHandlersValue,
   901→  });
   902→
   903→  return (
   904→    <ImageGenerationFormProvider value={contextValue}>
   905→      <form id="image-generation-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
   906→        {/* Main Content Layout */}
   907→        <div className="flex gap-6 flex-col md:flex-row pb-4">
   908→          {/* Left Column - Prompts and Shot Selector */}
   909→          <div className="flex-1 space-y-6">
   910→            <PromptsSection
   911→              onPromptModeChange={(mode) => {
   912→                markAsInteracted();
   913→                setEffectivePromptMode(mode);
   914→                // Auto-set imagesPerPrompt based on mode
   915→                if (mode === 'automated') {
   916→                  setImagesPerPrompt(8);
   917→                } else if (mode === 'managed') {
   918→                  setImagesPerPrompt(1);
   919→                }
   920→              }}
   921→            />
   922→
   923→            <ShotSelector
   924→              shots={shots}
   925→              associatedShotId={associatedShotId}
   926→              isGenerating={automatedSubmitButton.isSubmitting}
   927→              onChangeShot={handleShotChange}
   928→              onClearShot={() => {
   929→                markAsInteracted();
   930→                setAssociatedShotId(null);
   931→              }}
   932→              onOpenCreateShot={() => uiActions.setCreateShotModalOpen(true)}
   933→              onJumpToShot={navigateToShot}
   934→            />
   935→          </div>
   936→          
   937→          {/* Right Column - Reference Image and Settings */}
   938→          <ModelSection
   939→            // Props not in context
   940→            generationSource={generationSource}
   941→            onGenerationSourceChange={handleGenerationSourceChange}
   942→            selectedTextModel={selectedTextModel}
   943→            onTextModelChange={handleTextModelChange}
   944→            onOpenLoraModal={() => loraManager.setIsLoraModalOpen(true)}
   945→            isLoadingReferenceData={isReferenceDataLoading}
   946→            referenceCount={referenceCount}
   947→          />
   948→        </div>
   949→
   950→        {/* Generation Settings (dimensions always, phase config for local only) */}
   951→        <div className="md:col-span-2 mt-2">
   952→          <GenerationSettingsSection
   953→            hiresFixConfig={hiresFixConfig}
   954→            onHiresFixConfigChange={setHiresFixConfig}
   955→            projectResolution={projectResolution}
   956→            projectAspectRatio={projectAspectRatio}
   957→            disabled={automatedSubmitButton.isSubmitting}
   958→            isLocalGeneration={isLocalGenerationEnabled}
   959→          />
   960→        </div>
   961→
   962→        {/* Spacer to ensure content can scroll above sticky footer (only when not using portal) */}
   963→        {stickyFooter && !footerPortalId && <div className="h-4" />}
   964→
   965→        {/* Footer: portaled when footerPortalId provided, sticky when stickyFooter without portal, inline otherwise */}
   966→        {(() => {
   967→          const footerContent = (
   968→            <div className={
   969→              stickyFooter
   970→                ? footerPortalId
   971→                  ? "px-6 py-3 bg-background border-t border-zinc-700" // Portaled footer
   972→                  : "sticky bottom-0 z-50 !mt-0 -mx-6 px-6 py-3 bg-background border-t border-zinc-700" // Sticky footer
   973→                : "border-t border-border pt-6 mt-2" // Inline footer (tool page)
   974→            }>
   975→              <GenerateControls
   976→                imagesPerPrompt={imagesPerPrompt}
   977→                onChangeImagesPerPrompt={handleSliderChange(setImagesPerPrompt)}
   978→                actionablePromptsCount={actionablePromptsCount}
   979→                isGenerating={automatedSubmitButton.isSubmitting}
   980→                justQueued={automatedSubmitButton.isSuccess}
   981→                promptMode={effectivePromptMode}
   982→                onUseExistingPrompts={handleUseExistingPrompts}
   983→                onNewPromptsLikeExisting={handleNewPromptsLikeExisting}
   984→                promptMultiplier={promptMultiplier}
   985→                onChangePromptMultiplier={setPromptMultiplier}
   986→              />
   987→            </div>
   988→          );
   989→
   990→          // Portal footer outside scroll container when footerPortalId is provided
   991→          if (stickyFooter && footerPortalId) {
   992→            const portalTarget = document.getElementById(footerPortalId);
   993→            return portalTarget ? createPortal(footerContent, portalTarget) : footerContent;
   994→          }
   995→          return footerContent;
   996→        })()}
   997→      </form>
   998→
   999→      <ChunkLoadErrorBoundary>
  1000→        <Suspense fallback={<div className="sr-only">Loading...</div>}>
  1001→          <LazyLoraSelectorModal
  1002→            isOpen={loraManager.isLoraModalOpen}
  1003→            onClose={() => loraManager.setIsLoraModalOpen(false)}
  1004→            loras={availableLoras}
  1005→            onAddLora={handleAddLora}
  1006→            onRemoveLora={handleRemoveLora}
  1007→            onUpdateLoraStrength={handleLoraStrengthChange}
  1008→            selectedLoras={loraManager.selectedLoras.map(lora => {
  1009→              const fullLora = availableLoras.find(loraModel => loraModel['Model ID'] === lora.id);
  1010→              return {
  1011→                ...fullLora,
  1012→                "Model ID": lora.id,
  1013→                Name: lora.name,
  1014→                strength: lora.strength,
  1015→              } as LoraModel & { strength: number };
  1016→            })}
  1017→            lora_type={generationSource === 'just-text' ? getLoraTypeForModel(selectedTextModel) : BY_REFERENCE_LORA_TYPE}
  1018→          />
  1019→        </Suspense>
  1020→      </ChunkLoadErrorBoundary>
  1021→
  1022→      <ChunkLoadErrorBoundary>
  1023→        <Suspense fallback={<div className="sr-only">Loading...</div>}>
  1024→          <LazyPromptEditorModal
  1025→            isOpen={uiState.isPromptModalOpen}
  1026→            onClose={() => uiActions.closePromptModal()}
  1027→            prompts={prompts}
  1028→            onSave={handleSavePromptsFromModal}
  1029→            generatePromptId={generatePromptId}
  1030→            apiKey={openaiApiKey}
  1031→            openWithAIExpanded={uiState.openPromptModalWithAIExpanded}
  1032→            onGenerateAndQueue={handleGenerateAndQueue}
  1033→          />
  1034→        </Suspense>
  1035→      </ChunkLoadErrorBoundary>
  1036→
  1037→      <CreateShotModal
  1038→        isOpen={uiState.isCreateShotModalOpen}
  1039→        onClose={() => uiActions.setCreateShotModalOpen(false)}
  1040→        onSubmit={handleCreateShot}
  1041→        isLoading={isCreatingShot}
  1042→        projectId={selectedProjectId}
  1043→      />
  1044→    </ImageGenerationFormProvider>
  1045→  );
  1046→});
  1047→
  1048→ImageGenerationForm.displayName = 'ImageGenerationForm';

    43→  currentAfterPromptText: string;
    44→  currentStyleBoostTerms: string;
    45→  isLocalGenerationEnabled: boolean;
    46→  hiresFixConfig: HiresFixConfig;
    47→  effectivePromptMode: PromptMode;
    48→  masterPromptText: string;
    49→  actionablePromptsCount: number;
    50→
    51→  // Generation source
    52→  generationSourceRef: React.MutableRefObject<GenerationSource>;
    53→  selectedTextModelRef: React.MutableRefObject<TextToImageModel>;
    54→  styleReferenceImageGeneration: string | null;
    55→
    56→  // Reference settings
    57→  currentStyleStrength: number;
    58→  currentSubjectStrength: number;
    59→  effectiveSubjectDescription: string;
    60→  currentInThisScene: boolean;
    61→  currentInThisSceneStrength: number;
    62→  referenceMode: ReferenceMode;
    63→
    64→  // AI prompt generation
    65→  aiGeneratePrompts: (params: {
    66→    overallPromptText: string;
    67→    numberToGenerate: number;
    68→    existingPrompts?: Array<{ id: string; text: string; shortText?: string }>;
    69→    includeExistingContext?: boolean;
    70→    addSummaryForNewPrompts?: boolean;
    71→    replaceCurrentPrompts?: boolean;
    72→    temperature?: number;
    73→    rulesToRememberText?: string;
    74→  }) => Promise<Array<{ id: string; text: string; shortText?: string }>>;
    75→
    76→  // Task status for baseline tracking
    77→  taskStatusCounts: { processing: number } | undefined;
    78→
    79→  // Callbacks
    80→  onGenerate: (params: BatchImageGenerationTaskParams) => Promise<string[]> | string[] | void;
    81→  setPrompts: (prompts: PromptEntry[] | ((prev: PromptEntry[]) => PromptEntry[])) => void;
    82→
    83→  // Submit button state
    84→  automatedSubmitButton: {
    85→    trigger: () => void;
    86→    isSubmitting: boolean;
    87→    isSuccess: boolean;
    88→  };
    89→}
    90→
    91→interface UseFormSubmissionReturn {
    92→  // Handlers
    93→  getTaskParams: (
    94→    promptsToUse: PromptEntry[],
    95→    options?: { imagesPerPromptOverride?: number }
    96→  ) => BatchImageGenerationTaskParams | null;
    97→  handleSubmit: (e: React.FormEvent) => Promise<void>;
    98→  handleGenerateAndQueue: (updatedPrompts: PromptEntry[]) => void;
    99→  handleUseExistingPrompts: () => Promise<void>;
   100→  handleNewPromptsLikeExisting: () => Promise<void>;
   101→
   102→  // State
   103→  isGeneratingAutomatedPrompts: boolean;
   104→}
   105→
   106→// ============================================================================
   107→// Hook Implementation
   108→// ============================================================================
   109→
   110→export function useFormSubmission(props: UseFormSubmissionProps): UseFormSubmissionReturn {
   111→  const {
   112→    selectedProjectId,
   113→    prompts,
   114→    imagesPerPrompt,
   115→    promptMultiplier,
   116→    associatedShotId,
   117→    currentBeforePromptText,
   118→    currentAfterPromptText,
   119→    currentStyleBoostTerms,
   120→    isLocalGenerationEnabled,
   121→    hiresFixConfig,
   122→    effectivePromptMode,
   123→    masterPromptText,
   124→    actionablePromptsCount,
   125→    generationSourceRef,
   126→    selectedTextModelRef,
   127→    styleReferenceImageGeneration,
   128→    currentStyleStrength,
   129→    currentSubjectStrength,
   130→    effectiveSubjectDescription,
   131→    currentInThisScene,
   132→    currentInThisSceneStrength,
   133→    referenceMode,
   134→    aiGeneratePrompts,
   135→    taskStatusCounts,
   136→    onGenerate,
   137→    setPrompts,
   138→    automatedSubmitButton,
   139→  } = props;
   140→
   141→  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
   142→  const { addIncomingTask, completeIncomingTask } = useIncomingTasks();
   143→
   144→  // Track automated prompt generation state
   145→  const isGeneratingAutomatedPromptsRef = useRef(false);
   146→
   147→  // ============================================================================
   148→  // Build Task Params
   149→  // ============================================================================
   150→
   151→  const getTaskParams = useCallback((
   152→    promptsToUse: PromptEntry[],
   153→    options?: { imagesPerPromptOverride?: number }
   154→  ): BatchImageGenerationTaskParams | null => {
   155→    const activePrompts = promptsToUse.filter(p => p.fullPrompt.trim() !== "");
   156→
   157→    if (activePrompts.length === 0) {
   158→      toast.error("Please enter at least one valid prompt.");
   159→      return null;
   160→    }
   161→
   162→    const currentGenerationSource = generationSourceRef.current;
   163→    const currentTextModel = selectedTextModelRef.current;
   164→
   165→    // Validate: require style reference for by-reference mode
   166→    if (currentGenerationSource === 'by-reference' && !styleReferenceImageGeneration) {
   167→      toast.error("Please upload a style reference image for by-reference mode.");
   168→      return null;
   169→    }
   170→
   171→    // Only include reference params for by-reference mode
   172→    const referenceParams: ReferenceApiParams = currentGenerationSource === 'by-reference' ? {
   173→      style_reference_image: styleReferenceImageGeneration ?? undefined,
   174→      style_reference_strength: currentStyleStrength,
   175→      subject_strength: currentSubjectStrength,
   176→      subject_description: effectiveSubjectDescription,
   177→      in_this_scene: currentInThisScene,
   178→      in_this_scene_strength: currentInThisSceneStrength,
   179→      reference_mode: referenceMode,
   180→    } : {};
   181→
   182→    return buildBatchTaskParams({
   183→      projectId: selectedProjectId!,
   184→      prompts: activePrompts,
   185→      imagesPerPrompt: options?.imagesPerPromptOverride ?? imagesPerPrompt,
   186→      shotId: associatedShotId,
   187→      beforePromptText: currentBeforePromptText,
   188→      afterPromptText: currentAfterPromptText,
   189→      styleBoostTerms: currentStyleBoostTerms,
   190→      isLocalGenerationEnabled,
   191→      hiresFixConfig,
   192→      modelName: currentGenerationSource === 'just-text' ? currentTextModel : 'qwen-image',
   193→      referenceParams,
   194→    });
   195→  }, [
   196→    styleReferenceImageGeneration,
   197→    currentStyleStrength,
   198→    currentSubjectStrength,
   199→    effectiveSubjectDescription,
   200→    currentInThisScene,
   201→    currentInThisSceneStrength,
   202→    referenceMode,
   203→    selectedProjectId,
   204→    imagesPerPrompt,
   205→    associatedShotId,
   206→    currentBeforePromptText,
   207→    currentAfterPromptText,
   208→    currentStyleBoostTerms,
   209→    isLocalGenerationEnabled,
   210→    hiresFixConfig,
   211→    generationSourceRef,
   212→    selectedTextModelRef,
   213→  ]);
   214→
   215→  // ============================================================================
   216→  // Handle Submit
   217→  // ============================================================================
   218→
   219→  const handleSubmit = useCallback(async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
   220→    e.preventDefault();
   221→
   222→    // Handle automated mode: generate prompts first, then images
   223→    if (effectivePromptMode === 'automated') {
   224→      if (!masterPromptText.trim()) {
   225→        toast.error("Please enter a master prompt.");
   226→        return;
   227→      }
   228→
   229→      const currentGenerationSource = generationSourceRef.current;
   230→      const currentTextModel = selectedTextModelRef.current;
   231→
   232→      // Validate early
   233→      if (currentGenerationSource === 'by-reference' && !styleReferenceImageGeneration) {
   234→        toast.error("Please upload a style reference image for by-reference mode.");
   235→        return;
   236→      }
   237→
   238→      // Capture current values for background operation
   239→      const capturedMasterPrompt = masterPromptText;
   240→      const capturedImagesPerPrompt = imagesPerPrompt;
   241→      const capturedPromptMultiplier = promptMultiplier;
   242→      const capturedProjectId = selectedProjectId!;
   243→      const capturedAssociatedShotId = associatedShotId;
   244→      const capturedGenerationSource = currentGenerationSource;
   245→      const capturedStyleRef = styleReferenceImageGeneration;
   246→      const capturedStyleStrength = currentStyleStrength;
   247→      const capturedSubjectStrength = currentSubjectStrength;
   248→      const capturedSubjectDescription = effectiveSubjectDescription;
   249→      const capturedInThisScene = currentInThisScene;
   250→      const capturedInThisSceneStrength = currentInThisSceneStrength;
   251→      const capturedReferenceMode = referenceMode;
   252→      const capturedBeforePromptText = currentBeforePromptText;
   253→      const capturedAfterPromptText = currentAfterPromptText;
   254→      const capturedStyleBoostTerms = currentStyleBoostTerms;
   255→      const capturedIsLocalGenerationEnabled = isLocalGenerationEnabled;
   256→      const capturedHiresFixConfig = hiresFixConfig;
   257→      const capturedModelName = currentGenerationSource === 'just-text' ? currentTextModel : 'qwen-image';
   258→
   259→      // Trigger button state
   260→      automatedSubmitButton.trigger();
   261→
   262→      // Add incoming task filler
   263→      const truncatedPrompt = capturedMasterPrompt.length > 50
   264→        ? capturedMasterPrompt.substring(0, 50) + '...'
   265→        : capturedMasterPrompt;
   266→      const currentBaseline = taskStatusCounts?.processing ?? 0;
   267→      const incomingTaskId = addIncomingTask({
   268→        taskType: 'image_generation',
   269→        label: truncatedPrompt,
   270→        expectedCount: capturedImagesPerPrompt * capturedPromptMultiplier,
   271→        baselineCount: currentBaseline,
   272→      });
   273→
   274→      // Fire-and-forget background operation
   275→      (async () => {
   276→        try {
   277→          isGeneratingAutomatedPromptsRef.current = true;
   278→
   279→          const rawResults = await aiGeneratePrompts({
   280→            overallPromptText: capturedMasterPrompt,
   281→            numberToGenerate: capturedImagesPerPrompt,
   282→            includeExistingContext: false,
   283→            addSummaryForNewPrompts: true,
   284→            replaceCurrentPrompts: true,
   285→            temperature: 0.8,
   286→            rulesToRememberText: '',
   287→          });
   288→
   289→          const newPrompts: PromptEntry[] = rawResults.map(item => ({
   290→            id: item.id,
   291→            fullPrompt: item.text,
   292→            shortPrompt: item.shortText || item.text.substring(0, 30) + (item.text.length > 30 ? "..." : ""),
   293→          }));
   294→
   295→          setPrompts(newPrompts);
   296→
   297→          // Build task params
   298→          const referenceParams: ReferenceApiParams = capturedGenerationSource === 'by-reference' ? {
   299→            style_reference_image: capturedStyleRef ?? undefined,
   300→            style_reference_strength: capturedStyleStrength,
   301→            subject_strength: capturedSubjectStrength,
   302→            subject_description: capturedSubjectDescription,
   303→            in_this_scene: capturedInThisScene,
   304→            in_this_scene_strength: capturedInThisSceneStrength,
   305→            reference_mode: capturedReferenceMode,
   306→          } : {};
   307→
   308→          const taskParams = buildBatchTaskParams({
   309→            projectId: capturedProjectId,
   310→            prompts: newPrompts,
   311→            imagesPerPrompt: capturedPromptMultiplier,
   312→            shotId: capturedAssociatedShotId,
   313→            beforePromptText: capturedBeforePromptText,
   314→            afterPromptText: capturedAfterPromptText,
   315→            styleBoostTerms: capturedStyleBoostTerms,
   316→            isLocalGenerationEnabled: capturedIsLocalGenerationEnabled,
   317→            hiresFixConfig: capturedHiresFixConfig,
   318→            modelName: capturedModelName,
   319→            referenceParams,
   320→          });
   321→
   322→          await onGenerate(taskParams);
   323→        } catch (error) {
   324→          handleError(error, { context: 'useFormSubmission.handleSubmit.automatedMode', toastTitle: 'Failed to generate prompts. Please try again.' });
   325→        } finally {
   326→          isGeneratingAutomatedPromptsRef.current = false;
   327→          await queryClient.refetchQueries({ [REDACTED] });
   328→          await queryClient.refetchQueries({ [REDACTED] });
   329→          const newCount = queryClient.getQueryData<{ processing: number }>(queryKeys.tasks.statusCounts(selectedProjectId))?.processing ?? 0;
   330→          completeIncomingTask(incomingTaskId, newCount);
   331→        }
   332→      })();
   333→
   334→      return;
   335→    }
   336→
   337→    // Managed mode: use getTaskParams
   338→    const taskParams = getTaskParams(prompts);
   339→    if (!taskParams) return;
   340→
   341→    automatedSubmitButton.trigger();
   342→
   343→    // Add incoming task filler
   344→    const firstPrompt = prompts.find(p => p.fullPrompt.trim())?.fullPrompt || 'Generating...';
   345→    const truncatedPrompt = firstPrompt.length > 50
   346→      ? firstPrompt.substring(0, 50) + '...'
   347→      : firstPrompt;
   348→    const managedBaseline = taskStatusCounts?.processing ?? 0;
   349→    const incomingTaskId = addIncomingTask({
   350→      taskType: 'image_generation',
   351→      label: truncatedPrompt,
   352→      expectedCount: actionablePromptsCount * imagesPerPrompt,
   353→      baselineCount: managedBaseline,
   354→    });
   355→
   356→    // Fire-and-forget
   357→    (async () => {
   358→      try {
   359→        await onGenerate(taskParams);
   360→      } catch (error) {
   361→        handleError(error, { context: 'useFormSubmission.handleSubmit.managedMode', toastTitle: 'Failed to create tasks. Please try again.' });
   362→      } finally {
   363→        await queryClient.refetchQueries({ [REDACTED] });
   364→        await queryClient.refetchQueries({ [REDACTED] });
   365→        const newCount = queryClient.getQueryData<{ processing: number }>(queryKeys.tasks.statusCounts(selectedProjectId))?.processing ?? 0;
   366→        completeIncomingTask(incomingTaskId, newCount);
   367→      }
   368→    })();
   369→  }, [
   370→    effectivePromptMode,
   371→    masterPromptText,
   372→    generationSourceRef,
   373→    selectedTextModelRef,
   374→    styleReferenceImageGeneration,
   375→    imagesPerPrompt,
   376→    promptMultiplier,
   377→    selectedProjectId,
   378→    associatedShotId,
   379→    currentStyleStrength,
   380→    currentSubjectStrength,
   381→    effectiveSubjectDescription,
   382→    currentInThisScene,
   383→    currentInThisSceneStrength,
   384→    referenceMode,
   385→    currentBeforePromptText,
   386→    currentAfterPromptText,
   387→    currentStyleBoostTerms,
   388→    isLocalGenerationEnabled,
   389→    hiresFixConfig,
   390→    automatedSubmitButton,
   391→    taskStatusCounts,
   392→    addIncomingTask,
   393→    aiGeneratePrompts,
   394→    setPrompts,
   395→    onGenerate,
   396→    queryClient,
   397→    completeIncomingTask,
   398→    getTaskParams,
   399→    prompts,
   400→    actionablePromptsCount,
   401→  ]);
   402→
   403→  // ============================================================================
   404→  // Handle Generate And Queue
   405→  // ============================================================================
   406→
   407→  const handleGenerateAndQueue = useCallback((updatedPrompts: PromptEntry[]) => {
   408→
   409→    // Save prompts to state
   410→    const seenIds = new Set<string>();
   411→    const sanitizedPrompts = updatedPrompts.map(original => {
   412→      let id = original.id && !seenIds.has(original.id) ? original.id : "";
   413→      if (!id) {
   414→        id = `prompt-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9)}`;
   415→      }
   416→      seenIds.add(id);
   417→      return {
   418→        ...original,
   419→        id,
   420→        shortPrompt: original.shortPrompt || (original.fullPrompt.substring(0, 30) + (original.fullPrompt.length > 30 ? "..." : "")),
   421→      };
   422→    });
   423→
   424→    setPrompts(sanitizedPrompts);
   425→
   426→    // Build task params
   427→    const taskParams = getTaskParams(updatedPrompts);
   428→    if (!taskParams) return;
   429→
   430→    onGenerate(taskParams);
   431→  }, [setPrompts, getTaskParams, onGenerate]);
   432→
   433→  // ============================================================================
   434→  // Handle Use Existing Prompts
   435→  // ============================================================================
   436→
   437→  const handleUseExistingPrompts = useCallback(async () => {
   438→    const taskParams = getTaskParams(prompts, { imagesPerPromptOverride: promptMultiplier });
   439→    if (!taskParams) return;
   440→    onGenerate(taskParams);
   441→  }, [prompts, promptMultiplier, getTaskParams, onGenerate]);
   442→
   443→  // ============================================================================
   444→  // Handle New Prompts Like Existing
   445→  // ============================================================================
   446→
   447→  const handleNewPromptsLikeExisting = useCallback(async () => {
   448→    const activePrompts = prompts.filter(p => p.fullPrompt.trim() !== "");
   449→    if (activePrompts.length === 0) {
   450→      toast.error("No prompts available. Please add prompts first.");
   451→      return;
   452→    }
   453→
   454→    if (generationSourceRef.current === 'by-reference' && !styleReferenceImageGeneration) {
   455→      toast.error("Please upload a style reference image for by-reference mode.");
   456→      return;
   457→    }
   458→
   459→    try {
   460→      isGeneratingAutomatedPromptsRef.current = true;
   461→
   462→      const rawResults = await aiGeneratePrompts({
   463→        overallPromptText: "Make me more prompts like this.",
   464→        numberToGenerate: imagesPerPrompt,
   465→        existingPrompts: activePrompts.map(p => ({ id: p.id, text: p.fullPrompt, shortText: p.shortPrompt })),
   466→        addSummaryForNewPrompts: true,
   467→        replaceCurrentPrompts: true,
   468→        temperature: 0.8,
   469→        rulesToRememberText: '',
   470→      });
   471→
   472→      const newPrompts: PromptEntry[] = rawResults.map(item => ({
   473→        id: item.id,
   474→        fullPrompt: item.text,
   475→        shortPrompt: item.shortText || item.text.substring(0, 30) + (item.text.length > 30 ? "..." : ""),
   476→      }));
   477→
   478→      setPrompts(newPrompts);
   479→
   480→      const taskParams = getTaskParams(newPrompts, { imagesPerPromptOverride: promptMultiplier });
   481→      if (!taskParams) return;
   482→
   483→      onGenerate(taskParams);
   484→    } catch (error) {
   485→      handleError(error, { context: 'useFormSubmission.handleNewPromptsLikeExisting', toastTitle: 'Failed to generate prompts. Please try again.' });
   486→    } finally {
   487→      isGeneratingAutomatedPromptsRef.current = false;
   488→    }
   489→  }, [
   490→    prompts,
   491→    styleReferenceImageGeneration,
   492→    generationSourceRef,
   493→    imagesPerPrompt,
   494→    promptMultiplier,
   495→    getTaskParams,
   496→    onGenerate,
   497→    aiGeneratePrompts,
   498→    setPrompts,
   499→  ]);
   500→
   501→  // ============================================================================
   502→  // Return
   503→  // ============================================================================
   504→
   505→  return {
   506→    getTaskParams,
   507→    handleSubmit,
   508→    handleGenerateAndQueue,
   509→    handleUseExistingPrompts,
   510→    handleNewPromptsLikeExisting,
   511→    isGeneratingAutomatedPrompts: isGeneratingAutomatedPromptsRef.current,
   512→  };
   513→}

    62→  referenceMode: ReferenceMode;
    63→
    64→  // AI prompt generation
    65→  aiGeneratePrompts: (params: {
    66→    overallPromptText: string;
    67→    numberToGenerate: number;
    68→    existingPrompts?: Array<{ id: string; text: string; shortText?: string }>;
    69→    includeExistingContext?: boolean;
    70→    addSummaryForNewPrompts?: boolean;
    71→    replaceCurrentPrompts?: boolean;
    72→    temperature?: number;
    73→    rulesToRememberText?: string;
    74→  }) => Promise<Array<{ id: string; text: string; shortText?: string }>>;
    75→
    76→  // Callbacks
    77→  onGenerate: (params: BatchImageGenerationTaskParams) => Promise<string[]> | string[] | void;
    78→  setPrompts: (prompts: PromptEntry[] | ((prev: PromptEntry[]) => PromptEntry[])) => void;
    79→
    80→  // Submit button state
    81→  automatedSubmitButton: {
    82→    trigger: () => void;
    83→    isSubmitting: boolean;
    84→    isSuccess: boolean;
    85→  };
    86→}
    87→
    88→interface UseFormSubmissionReturn {
    89→  // Handlers
    90→  getTaskParams: (
    91→    promptsToUse: PromptEntry[],
    92→    options?: { imagesPerPromptOverride?: number }
    93→  ) => BatchImageGenerationTaskParams | null;
    94→  handleSubmit: (e: React.FormEvent) => Promise<void>;
    95→  handleGenerateAndQueue: (updatedPrompts: PromptEntry[]) => void;
    96→  handleUseExistingPrompts: () => Promise<void>;
    97→  handleNewPromptsLikeExisting: () => Promise<void>;
    98→
    99→  // State
   100→  isGeneratingAutomatedPrompts: boolean;
   101→}
   102→
   103→// ============================================================================
   104→// Hook Implementation
   105→// ============================================================================
   106→
   107→export function useFormSubmission(props: UseFormSubmissionProps): UseFormSubmissionReturn {
   108→  const {
   109→    selectedProjectId,
   110→    prompts,
   111→    imagesPerPrompt,
   112→    promptMultiplier,
   113→    associatedShotId,
   114→    currentBeforePromptText,
   115→    currentAfterPromptText,
   116→    currentStyleBoostTerms,
   117→    isLocalGenerationEnabled,
   118→    hiresFixConfig,
   119→    effectivePromptMode,
   120→    masterPromptText,
   121→    actionablePromptsCount,
   122→    generationSourceRef,
   123→    selectedTextModelRef,
   124→    styleReferenceImageGeneration,
   125→    currentStyleStrength,
   126→    currentSubjectStrength,
   127→    effectiveSubjectDescription,
   128→    currentInThisScene,
   129→    currentInThisSceneStrength,
   130→    referenceMode,
   131→    aiGeneratePrompts,
   132→    onGenerate,
   133→    setPrompts,
   134→    automatedSubmitButton,
   135→  } = props;
   136→
   137→  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
   138→  const { addIncomingTask, completeIncomingTask } = useIncomingTasks();
   139→
   140→  // Track automated prompt generation state
   141→  const isGeneratingAutomatedPromptsRef = useRef(false);
   142→
   143→  // ============================================================================
   144→  // Build Task Params
   145→  // ============================================================================
   146→
   147→  const getTaskParams = useCallback((
   148→    promptsToUse: PromptEntry[],
   149→    options?: { imagesPerPromptOverride?: number }
   150→  ): BatchImageGenerationTaskParams | null => {
   151→    const activePrompts = promptsToUse.filter(p => p.fullPrompt.trim() !== "");
   152→
   153→    if (activePrompts.length === 0) {
   154→      toast.error("Please enter at least one valid prompt.");
   155→      return null;
   156→    }
   157→
   158→    const currentGenerationSource = generationSourceRef.current;
   159→    const currentTextModel = selectedTextModelRef.current;
   160→
   161→    // Validate: require style reference for by-reference mode
   162→    if (currentGenerationSource === 'by-reference' && !styleReferenceImageGeneration) {
   163→      toast.error("Please upload a style reference image for by-reference mode.");
   164→      return null;
   165→    }
   166→
   167→    // Only include reference params for by-reference mode
   168→    const referenceParams: ReferenceApiParams = currentGenerationSource === 'by-reference' ? {
   169→      style_reference_image: styleReferenceImageGeneration ?? undefined,
   170→      style_reference_strength: currentStyleStrength,
   171→      subject_strength: currentSubjectStrength,
   172→      subject_description: effectiveSubjectDescription,
   173→      in_this_scene: currentInThisScene,
   174→      in_this_scene_strength: currentInThisSceneStrength,
   175→      reference_mode: referenceMode,
   176→    } : {};
   177→
   178→    return buildBatchTaskParams({
   179→      projectId: selectedProjectId!,
   180→      prompts: activePrompts,
   181→      imagesPerPrompt: options?.imagesPerPromptOverride ?? imagesPerPrompt,
   182→      shotId: associatedShotId,
   183→      beforePromptText: currentBeforePromptText,
   184→      afterPromptText: currentAfterPromptText,
   185→      styleBoostTerms: currentStyleBoostTerms,
   186→      isLocalGenerationEnabled,
   187→      hiresFixConfig,
   188→      modelName: currentGenerationSource === 'just-text' ? currentTextModel : 'qwen-image',
   189→      referenceParams,
   190→    });
   191→  }, [
   192→    styleReferenceImageGeneration,
   193→    currentStyleStrength,
   194→    currentSubjectStrength,
   195→    effectiveSubjectDescription,
   196→    currentInThisScene,
   197→    currentInThisSceneStrength,
   198→    referenceMode,
   199→    selectedProjectId,
   200→    imagesPerPrompt,
   201→    associatedShotId,
   202→    currentBeforePromptText,
   203→    currentAfterPromptText,
   204→    currentStyleBoostTerms,
   205→    isLocalGenerationEnabled,
   206→    hiresFixConfig,
   207→    generationSourceRef,
   208→    selectedTextModelRef,
   209→  ]);
   210→
   211→  // ============================================================================
   212→  // Handle Submit
   213→  // ============================================================================
   214→
   215→  const handleSubmit = useCallback(async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
   216→    e.preventDefault();
   217→
   218→    // Handle automated mode: generate prompts first, then images
   219→    if (effectivePromptMode === 'automated') {
   220→      if (!masterPromptText.trim()) {
   221→        toast.error("Please enter a master prompt.");
   222→        return;
   223→      }
   224→
   225→      const currentGenerationSource = generationSourceRef.current;
   226→      const currentTextModel = selectedTextModelRef.current;
   227→
   228→      // Validate early
   229→      if (currentGenerationSource === 'by-reference' && !styleReferenceImageGeneration) {
   230→        toast.error("Please upload a style reference image for by-reference mode.");
   231→        return;
   232→      }
   233→
   234→      // Capture current values for background operation
   235→      const capturedMasterPrompt = masterPromptText;
   236→      const capturedImagesPerPrompt = imagesPerPrompt;
   237→      const capturedPromptMultiplier = promptMultiplier;
   238→      const capturedProjectId = selectedProjectId!;
   239→      const capturedAssociatedShotId = associatedShotId;
   240→      const capturedGenerationSource = currentGenerationSource;
   241→      const capturedStyleRef = styleReferenceImageGeneration;
   242→      const capturedStyleStrength = currentStyleStrength;
   243→      const capturedSubjectStrength = currentSubjectStrength;
   244→      const capturedSubjectDescription = effectiveSubjectDescription;
   245→      const capturedInThisScene = currentInThisScene;
   246→      const capturedInThisSceneStrength = currentInThisSceneStrength;
   247→      const capturedReferenceMode = referenceMode;
   248→      const capturedBeforePromptText = currentBeforePromptText;
   249→      const capturedAfterPromptText = currentAfterPromptText;
   250→      const capturedStyleBoostTerms = currentStyleBoostTerms;
   251→      const capturedIsLocalGenerationEnabled = isLocalGenerationEnabled;
   252→      const capturedHiresFixConfig = hiresFixConfig;
   253→      const capturedModelName = currentGenerationSource === 'just-text' ? currentTextModel : 'qwen-image';
   254→
   255→      // Trigger button state
   256→      automatedSubmitButton.trigger();
   257→
   258→      // Add incoming task filler
   259→      const truncatedPrompt = capturedMasterPrompt.length > 50
   260→        ? capturedMasterPrompt.substring(0, 50) + '...'
   261→        : capturedMasterPrompt;
   262→      const currentBaseline = 0;
   263→      const incomingTaskId = addIncomingTask({
   264→        taskType: 'image_generation',
   265→        label: truncatedPrompt,
   266→        expectedCount: capturedImagesPerPrompt * capturedPromptMultiplier,
   267→        baselineCount: currentBaseline,
   268→      });
   269→
   270→      // Fire-and-forget background operation
   271→      (async () => {
   272→        try {
   273→          isGeneratingAutomatedPromptsRef.current = true;
   274→
   275→          const rawResults = await aiGeneratePrompts({
   276→            overallPromptText: capturedMasterPrompt,
   277→            numberToGenerate: capturedImagesPerPrompt,
   278→            includeExistingContext: false,
   279→            addSummaryForNewPrompts: true,
   280→            replaceCurrentPrompts: true,
   281→            temperature: 0.8,
   282→            rulesToRememberText: '',
   283→          });
   284→
   285→          const newPrompts: PromptEntry[] = rawResults.map(item => ({
   286→            id: item.id,
   287→            fullPrompt: item.text,
   288→            shortPrompt: item.shortText || item.text.substring(0, 30) + (item.text.length > 30 ? "..." : ""),
   289→          }));
   290→
   291→          setPrompts(newPrompts);
   292→
   293→          // Build task params
   294→          const referenceParams: ReferenceApiParams = capturedGenerationSource === 'by-reference' ? {
   295→            style_reference_image: capturedStyleRef ?? undefined,
   296→            style_reference_strength: capturedStyleStrength,
   297→            subject_strength: capturedSubjectStrength,
   298→            subject_description: capturedSubjectDescription,
   299→            in_this_scene: capturedInThisScene,
   300→            in_this_scene_strength: capturedInThisSceneStrength,
   301→            reference_mode: capturedReferenceMode,
   302→          } : {};
   303→
   304→          const taskParams = buildBatchTaskParams({
   305→            projectId: capturedProjectId,
   306→            prompts: newPrompts,
   307→            imagesPerPrompt: capturedPromptMultiplier,
   308→            shotId: capturedAssociatedShotId,
   309→            beforePromptText: capturedBeforePromptText,
   310→            afterPromptText: capturedAfterPromptText,
   311→            styleBoostTerms: capturedStyleBoostTerms,
   312→            isLocalGenerationEnabled: capturedIsLocalGenerationEnabled,
   313→            hiresFixConfig: capturedHiresFixConfig,
   314→            modelName: capturedModelName,
   315→            referenceParams,
   316→          });
   317→
   318→          await onGenerate(taskParams);
   319→        } catch (error) {
   320→          handleError(error, { context: 'useFormSubmission.handleSubmit.automatedMode', toastTitle: 'Failed to generate prompts. Please try again.' });
   321→        } finally {
   322→          isGeneratingAutomatedPromptsRef.current = false;
   323→          await queryClient.refetchQueries({ [REDACTED] });
   324→          await queryClient.refetchQueries({ [REDACTED] });
   325→          const newCount = queryClient.getQueryData<{ processing: number }>(queryKeys.tasks.statusCounts(selectedProjectId))?.processing ?? 0;
   326→          completeIncomingTask(incomingTaskId, newCount);
   327→        }
   328→      })();
   329→
   330→      return;
   331→    }
   332→
   333→    // Managed mode: use getTaskParams
   334→    const taskParams = getTaskParams(prompts);
   335→    if (!taskParams) return;
   336→
   337→    automatedSubmitButton.trigger();
   338→
   339→    // Add incoming task filler
   340→    const firstPrompt = prompts.find(p => p.fullPrompt.trim())?.fullPrompt || 'Generating...';
   341→    const truncatedPrompt = firstPrompt.length > 50
   342→      ? firstPrompt.substring(0, 50) + '...'
   343→      : firstPrompt;
   344→    const managedBaseline = 0;
   345→    const incomingTaskId = addIncomingTask({
   346→      taskType: 'image_generation',
   347→      label: truncatedPrompt,
   348→      expectedCount: actionablePromptsCount * imagesPerPrompt,
   349→      baselineCount: managedBaseline,
   350→    });
   351→
   352→    // Fire-and-forget
   353→    (async () => {
   354→      try {
   355→        await onGenerate(taskParams);
   356→      } catch (error) {
   357→        handleError(error, { context: 'useFormSubmission.handleSubmit.managedMode', toastTitle: 'Failed to create tasks. Please try again.' });
   358→      } finally {
   359→        await queryClient.refetchQueries({ [REDACTED] });
   360→        await queryClient.refetchQueries({ [REDACTED] });
   361→        const newCount = queryClient.getQueryData<{ processing: number }>(queryKeys.tasks.statusCounts(selectedProjectId))?.processing ?? 0;
   362→        completeIncomingTask(incomingTaskId, newCount);
   363→      }
   364→    })();
   365→  }, [
   366→    effectivePromptMode,
   367→    masterPromptText,
   368→    generationSourceRef,
   369→    selectedTextModelRef,
   370→    styleReferenceImageGeneration,
   371→    imagesPerPrompt,
   372→    promptMultiplier,
   373→    selectedProjectId,
   374→    associatedShotId,
   375→    currentStyleStrength,
   376→    currentSubjectStrength,
   377→    effectiveSubjectDescription,
   378→    currentInThisScene,
   379→    currentInThisSceneStrength,
   380→    referenceMode,
   381→    currentBeforePromptText,
   382→    currentAfterPromptText,
   383→    currentStyleBoostTerms,
   384→    isLocalGenerationEnabled,
   385→    hiresFixConfig,
   386→    automatedSubmitButton,
   387→    addIncomingTask,
   388→    aiGeneratePrompts,
   389→    setPrompts,
   390→    onGenerate,
   391→    queryClient,
   392→    completeIncomingTask,
   393→    getTaskParams,
   394→    prompts,
   395→    actionablePromptsCount,
   396→  ]);
   397→
   398→  // ============================================================================
   399→  // Handle Generate And Queue
   400→  // ============================================================================
   401→
   402→  const handleGenerateAndQueue = useCallback((updatedPrompts: PromptEntry[]) => {
   403→
   404→    // Save prompts to state
   405→    const seenIds = new Set<string>();
   406→    const sanitizedPrompts = updatedPrompts.map(original => {
   407→      let id = original.id && !seenIds.has(original.id) ? original.id : "";
   408→      if (!id) {
   409→        id = `prompt-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9)}`;
   410→      }
   411→      seenIds.add(id);
   412→      return {
   413→        ...original,
   414→        id,
   415→        shortPrompt: original.shortPrompt || (original.fullPrompt.substring(0, 30) + (original.fullPrompt.length > 30 ? "..." : "")),
   416→      };
   417→    });
   418→
   419→    setPrompts(sanitizedPrompts);
   420→
   421→    // Build task params
   422→    const taskParams = getTaskParams(updatedPrompts);
   423→    if (!taskParams) return;
   424→
   425→    onGenerate(taskParams);
   426→  }, [setPrompts, getTaskParams, onGenerate]);
   427→
   428→  // ============================================================================
   429→  // Handle Use Existing Prompts
   430→  // ============================================================================
   431→
   432→  const handleUseExistingPrompts = useCallback(async () => {
   433→    const taskParams = getTaskParams(prompts, { imagesPerPromptOverride: promptMultiplier });
   434→    if (!taskParams) return;
   435→    onGenerate(taskParams);
   436→  }, [prompts, promptMultiplier, getTaskParams, onGenerate]);
   437→
   438→  // ============================================================================
   439→  // Handle New Prompts Like Existing
   440→  // ============================================================================
   441→
   442→  const handleNewPromptsLikeExisting = useCallback(async () => {
   443→    const activePrompts = prompts.filter(p => p.fullPrompt.trim() !== "");
   444→    if (activePrompts.length === 0) {
   445→      toast.error("No prompts available. Please add prompts first.");
   446→      return;
   447→    }
   448→
   449→    if (generationSourceRef.current === 'by-reference' && !styleReferenceImageGeneration) {
   450→      toast.error("Please upload a style reference image for by-reference mode.");
   451→      return;
   452→    }
   453→
   454→    try {
   455→      isGeneratingAutomatedPromptsRef.current = true;
   456→
   457→      const rawResults = await aiGeneratePrompts({
   458→        overallPromptText: "Make me more prompts like this.",
   459→        numberToGenerate: imagesPerPrompt,
   460→        existingPrompts: activePrompts.map(p => ({ id: p.id, text: p.fullPrompt, shortText: p.shortPrompt })),
   461→        addSummaryForNewPrompts: true,
   462→        replaceCurrentPrompts: true,
   463→        temperature: 0.8,
   464→        rulesToRememberText: '',
   465→      });
   466→
   467→      const newPrompts: PromptEntry[] = rawResults.map(item => ({
   468→        id: item.id,
   469→        fullPrompt: item.text,
   470→        shortPrompt: item.shortText || item.text.substring(0, 30) + (item.text.length > 30 ? "..." : ""),
   471→      }));
   472→
   473→      setPrompts(newPrompts);
   474→
   475→      const taskParams = getTaskParams(newPrompts, { imagesPerPromptOverride: promptMultiplier });
   476→      if (!taskParams) return;
   477→
   478→      onGenerate(taskParams);
   479→    } catch (error) {
   480→      handleError(error, { context: 'useFormSubmission.handleNewPromptsLikeExisting', toastTitle: 'Failed to generate prompts. Please try again.' });
   481→    } finally {
   482→      isGeneratingAutomatedPromptsRef.current = false;
   483→    }
   484→  }, [
   485→    prompts,
   486→    styleReferenceImageGeneration,
   487→    generationSourceRef,
   488→    imagesPerPrompt,
   489→    promptMultiplier,
   490→    getTaskParams,
   491→    onGenerate,
   492→    aiGeneratePrompts,
   493→    setPrompts,
   494→  ]);
   495→
   496→  // ============================================================================
   497→  // Return
   498→  // ============================================================================
   499→
   500→  return {
   501→    getTaskParams,
   502→    handleSubmit,
   503→    handleGenerateAndQueue,
   504→    handleUseExistingPrompts,
   505→    handleNewPromptsLikeExisting,
   506→    isGeneratingAutomatedPrompts: isGeneratingAutomatedPromptsRef.current,
   507→  };
   508→}
   509→

The `handleUpdateReferenceName` and `handleToggleVisibility` handlers both reconstruct the full `StyleReferenceMetadata` from a `HydratedReferenceImage`. This is fragile - if new fields are added, these handlers forget to include them.

**Task:**

1. Read the file to find:
   - The `StyleReferenceMetadata` type (it may be imported or defined inline - find where it comes from)
   - The `handleUpdateReferenceName` handler - find how it builds metadata
   - The `handleToggleVisibility` handler - find how it builds metadata
   - The `HydratedReferenceImage` type fields

2. Add a helper function near the top of the file (after imports, before the hook):

```typescript
function buildResourceMetadata(
  ref: HydratedReferenceImage,
  overrides: Partial<StyleReferenceMetadata> = {},
): StyleReferenceMetadata {
  return {
    name: ref.name,
    styleReferenceImage: ref.styleReferenceImage,
    styleReferenceImageOriginal: ref.styleReferenceImageOriginal,
    thumbnailUrl: ref.thumbnailUrl,
    styleReferenceStrength: ref.styleReferenceStrength,
    subjectStrength: ref.subjectStrength,
    subjectDescription: ref.subjectDescription,
    inThisScene: ref.inThisScene,
    inThisSceneStrength: ref.inThisSceneStrength,
    referenceMode: ref.referenceMode,
    styleBoostTerms: ref.styleBoostTerms,
    created_by: { is_you: true },
    is_public: ref.isPublic,
    createdAt: ref.createdAt,
    updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    ...overrides,
  };
}
```

Note: Adjust `StyleReferenceMetadata` to whatever the actual type name is used in the file. It might be an inline object or a type imported from somewhere.

3. Replace both handlers to use the helper. The pattern should be:
```typescript
// handleUpdateReferenceName
const metadata = buildResourceMetadata(hydratedRef, { name });
await updateStyleReference.mutateAsync({ id: hydratedRef.resourceId, type: 'style-reference', metadata });

// handleToggleVisibility  
const metadata = buildResourceMetadata(hydratedRef, { is_public: !currentIsPublic });
await updateStyleReference.mutateAsync({ id: resourceId, type: 'style-reference', metadata });
```

   110→
   111→export function useReferenceManagement(props: UseReferenceManagementProps): UseReferenceManagementReturn {
   112→  const {
   113→    selectedProjectId,
   114→    effectiveShotId,
   115→    selectedReferenceId,
   116→    selectedReferenceIdByShot,
   117→    referencePointers,
   118→    hydratedReferences,
