   126→    shotPromptSettings,
   127→    setHiresFixConfig,
   128→  } = props;
   129→
   130→  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
   131→
   132→  // Resource mutation hooks
   133→  const createStyleReference = useCreateResource();
   134→  const updateStyleReference = useUpdateResource();
   135→  const deleteStyleReference = useDeleteResource();
   136→
   137→  // ============================================================================
   138→  // Local UI State
   139→  // ============================================================================
   140→
   141→  const [styleReferenceStrength, setStyleReferenceStrength] = useState<number>(1.0);
   142→  const [subjectStrength, setSubjectStrength] = useState<number>(0.0);
   143→  const [subjectDescription, setSubjectDescription] = useState<string>('');
   144→  const [isEditingSubjectDescription, setIsEditingSubjectDescription] = useState<boolean>(false);
   145→  const [inThisScene, setInThisScene] = useState<boolean>(false);
   146→  const [inThisSceneStrength, setInThisSceneStrength] = useState<number>(0.5);
   147→  const [referenceMode, setReferenceMode] = useState<ReferenceMode>('style');
   148→  const [styleBoostTerms, setStyleBoostTerms] = useState<string>('');
   149→  const [isUploadingStyleReference, setIsUploadingStyleReference] = useState<boolean>(false);
   150→  const [styleReferenceOverride, setStyleReferenceOverride] = useState<string | null | undefined>(undefined);
   151→
   152→  // Pending mode update tracking
   153→  const pendingReferenceModeUpdate = useRef<ReferenceMode | null>(null);
   154→
   155→  // ============================================================================
   156→  // Derived Values from Selected Reference
   157→  // ============================================================================
   158→
   159→  // Get current values from the selected reference (for backward compatibility)
   160→  const rawStyleReferenceImage = selectedReference?.styleReferenceImage || null;
   161→  const rawStyleReferenceImageOriginal = selectedReference?.styleReferenceImageOriginal || null;
   162→
   163→  // Display image (use original if available, fallback to processed)
   164→  const styleReferenceImageDisplay = useMemo(() => {
   165→    // If we have an explicit local override (including null), use it
   166→    if (styleReferenceOverride !== undefined) {
   167→      return styleReferenceOverride;
   168→    }
   169→
   170→    // Prefer original image for display
   171→    const imageToDisplay = rawStyleReferenceImageOriginal || rawStyleReferenceImage;
   172→    if (!imageToDisplay) return null;
   173→
   174→    // If it's already a URL, return as-is
   175→    if (imageToDisplay.startsWith('http')) {
   176→      return imageToDisplay;
   177→    }
   178→
   179→    // If it's base64 data, return null to trigger re-upload
   180→    if (imageToDisplay.startsWith('data:image/')) {
   181→      return null;
   182→    }
   183→
   184→    return imageToDisplay;
   185→  }, [styleReferenceOverride, rawStyleReferenceImageOriginal, rawStyleReferenceImage]);
   186→
   187→  // Generation image (always use processed version)
   188→  const styleReferenceImageGeneration = useMemo(() => {
   189→    if (!rawStyleReferenceImage) return null;
   190→
   191→    // If it's already a URL, return as-is
   192→    if (rawStyleReferenceImage.startsWith('http')) {
   193→      return rawStyleReferenceImage;
   194→    }
   195→
   196→    // If it's base64 data, return null
   197→    if (rawStyleReferenceImage.startsWith('data:image/')) {
   198→      return null;
   199→    }
   200→
   201→    return rawStyleReferenceImage;
   202→  }, [rawStyleReferenceImage]);
   203→
   204→  // ============================================================================
   205→  // Sync Effects
   206→  // ============================================================================
   207→
   208→  // When the backing setting updates, drop the local override
   209→  useEffect(() => {
   210→    setStyleReferenceOverride(undefined);
   211→  }, [rawStyleReferenceImage]);
   212→
   213→  // Clear pending mode update when switching references
   214→  const prevSelectedReferenceId = useRef(selectedReferenceId);
   215→  useEffect(() => {
   216→    if (prevSelectedReferenceId.current !== selectedReferenceId) {
   217→      pendingReferenceModeUpdate.current = null;
   218→      prevSelectedReferenceId.current = selectedReferenceId;
   219→    }
   220→  }, [selectedReferenceId]);
   221→
   222→  // Check if database has caught up with pending mode update
   223→  useEffect(() => {
   224→    const currentReferenceMode = selectedReference?.referenceMode || 'style';
   225→    if (pendingReferenceModeUpdate.current && currentReferenceMode === pendingReferenceModeUpdate.current) {
   226→      pendingReferenceModeUpdate.current = null;
   227→    }
   228→  }, [selectedReference?.referenceMode]);
   229→
   230→  // Sync local state from selectedReference when reference changes
   231→  const lastSyncedReferenceId = useRef<string | null>(null);
   232→  useEffect(() => {
   233→    // Only sync when reference ID actually changes (not on every re-render)
   234→    if (selectedReference && selectedReference.id !== lastSyncedReferenceId.current) {
   235→      lastSyncedReferenceId.current = selectedReference.id;
   236→
   237→      // Sync all reference settings to local state
   238→      setReferenceMode(selectedReference.referenceMode || 'style');
   239→      setStyleReferenceStrength(selectedReference.styleReferenceStrength ?? 1.0);
   240→      setSubjectStrength(selectedReference.subjectStrength ?? 0.0);
   241→      setSubjectDescription(selectedReference.subjectDescription ?? '');
   242→      setInThisScene(selectedReference.inThisScene ?? false);
   243→      setInThisSceneStrength(selectedReference.inThisSceneStrength ?? 0.5);
   244→      setStyleBoostTerms(selectedReference.styleBoostTerms ?? '');
   245→    }
   246→  }, [selectedReference]);
   247→
   248→  // ============================================================================
   249→  // Core Reference Update Handler
   250→  // ============================================================================
   251→
   252→  const handleUpdateReference = useCallback(async (referenceId: string, updates: Partial<HydratedReferenceImage>) => {
   253→
   254→    // Find the current pointer
   255→    const currentPointer = referencePointers.find(r => r.id === referenceId);
   256→    if (!currentPointer) {
   257→      console.error('[useReferenceManagement] Could not find reference pointer:', referenceId);
   258→      return;
   259→    }
   260→
   261→    // Update only the project-specific usage settings in the pointer
   262→    const updatedPointer: ReferenceImage = {
   263→      ...currentPointer,
   264→      ...(updates.referenceMode !== undefined && { referenceMode: updates.referenceMode }),
   265→      ...(updates.styleReferenceStrength !== undefined && { styleReferenceStrength: updates.styleReferenceStrength }),
   266→      ...(updates.subjectStrength !== undefined && { subjectStrength: updates.subjectStrength }),
   267→      ...(updates.subjectDescription !== undefined && { subjectDescription: updates.subjectDescription }),
   268→      ...(updates.inThisScene !== undefined && { inThisScene: updates.inThisScene }),
   269→      ...(updates.inThisSceneStrength !== undefined && { inThisSceneStrength: updates.inThisSceneStrength }),
   270→      ...(updates.styleBoostTerms !== undefined && { styleBoostTerms: updates.styleBoostTerms }),
   271→    };
   272→
   273→    // Update the references array in project settings
   274→    const updatedReferences = referencePointers.map(ref =>
   275→      ref.id === referenceId ? updatedPointer : ref
   276→    );
   277→
   278→    try {
   279→      await updateProjectImageSettings('project', {
   280→        references: updatedReferences,
   281→      });
   282→    } catch (error) {
   283→      handleError(error, { context: 'useReferenceManagement.handleUpdateReference', toastTitle: 'Failed to update reference settings' });
   284→    }
   285→
   286→    markAsInteracted();
   287→  }, [referencePointers, updateProjectImageSettings, markAsInteracted]);
   288→
   289→  // ============================================================================
   290→  // Handler: Upload New Reference
   291→  // ============================================================================
   292→
   293→  const handleStyleReferenceUpload = useCallback(async (files: File[]) => {
   294→    if (files.length === 0) return;
   295→
   296→    // GUARD: Don't add references while settings are loading
   297→    if (isLoadingProjectSettings) {
   298→      toast.error('Please wait for settings to load');
   299→      return;
   300→    }
   301→
   302→    const file = files[0];
   303→    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
   304→      return;
   305→    }
   306→
   307→    try {
   308→      setIsUploadingStyleReference(true);
   309→      const dataURL = await fileToDataURL(file);
   310→
   311→      // Upload the original image first (for display purposes)
   312→      const originalUploadedUrl = await uploadImageToStorage(file);
   313→
   314→      // Generate and upload thumbnail for grid display
   315→      let thumbnailUrl: string | null = null;
   316→      try {
   317→        const thumbnailResult = await generateClientThumbnail(file, 300, 0.8);
   318→
   319→        // Upload thumbnail to storage
   320→        const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
   321→        if (!session?.user?.id) {
   322→          throw new Error('User not authenticated');
   323→        }
   324→        const thumbnailFilename = generateThumbnailFilename();
   325→        const thumbnailPath = storagePaths.thumbnail(session.user.id, thumbnailFilename);
   326→
   327→        const { error: thumbnailUploadError } = await supabase.storage
   328→          .from(MEDIA_BUCKET)
   329→          .upload(thumbnailPath, thumbnailResult.thumbnailBlob, {
   330→            contentType: 'image/jpeg',
   331→            upsert: true
   332→          });
   333→
   334→        if (thumbnailUploadError) {
   335→          handleError(thumbnailUploadError, { context: 'useReferenceManagement.thumbnailUpload', showToast: false });
   336→          thumbnailUrl = originalUploadedUrl;
   337→        } else {
   338→          const { data: thumbnailUrlData } = supabase.storage
   339→            .from(MEDIA_BUCKET)
   340→            .getPublicUrl(thumbnailPath);
   341→          thumbnailUrl = thumbnailUrlData.publicUrl;
   342→        }
   343→      } catch (thumbnailError) {
   344→        handleError(thumbnailError, { context: 'useReferenceManagement.thumbnailGeneration', showToast: false });
   345→        thumbnailUrl = originalUploadedUrl;
   346→      }
   347→
   348→      // Process the image to match project aspect ratio (for generation)
   349→      let processedDataURL = dataURL;
   350→      if (selectedProjectId) {
   351→        const { aspectRatio } = await resolveProjectResolution(selectedProjectId);
   352→        const processed = await processStyleReferenceForAspectRatioString(dataURL, aspectRatio);
   353→
   354→        if (processed) {
   355→          processedDataURL = processed;
   356→        } else {
   357→          throw new Error('Failed to process image for aspect ratio');
   358→        }
   359→      }
   360→
   361→      // Convert processed data URL back to File for upload
   362→      const processedFile = dataURLtoFile(processedDataURL, `style-reference-processed-${Date.now()}.png`);
   363→      if (!processedFile) {
   364→        throw new Error('Failed to convert processed image to file');
   365→      }
   366→
   367→      // Upload processed version to storage
   368→      const processedUploadedUrl = await uploadImageToStorage(processedFile);
   369→
   370→      // Get user for metadata
   371→      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
   372→      if (!user) throw new Error('Not authenticated');
   373→
   374→      // Create resource metadata
   375→      const now = new Date().toISOString();
   376→      const metadata: StyleReferenceMetadata = {
   377→        name: `Reference ${(hydratedReferences.length + 1)}`,
   378→        styleReferenceImage: processedUploadedUrl,
   379→        styleReferenceImageOriginal: originalUploadedUrl,
   380→        thumbnailUrl: thumbnailUrl,
   381→        styleReferenceStrength: 1.1,
   382→        subjectStrength: 0.0,
   383→        subjectDescription: "",
   384→        inThisScene: false,
   385→        inThisSceneStrength: 1.0,
   386→        referenceMode: 'style',
   387→        styleBoostTerms: '',
   388→        is_public: privacyDefaults.resourcesPublic,
   389→        created_by: {
   390→          is_you: true,
   391→          username: user.email || 'user',
   392→        },
   393→        createdAt: now,
   394→        updatedAt: now,
   395→      };
   396→
   397→      // Create resource in resources table
   398→      const resource = await createStyleReference.mutateAsync({
   399→        type: 'style-reference',
   400→        metadata,
   401→      });
   402→
   403→      // Create lightweight pointer
   404→      const newPointer: ReferenceImage = {
   405→        id: nanoid(),
   406→        resourceId: resource.id,
   407→        createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
   408→      };
   409→
   410→      // Optimistic UI updates
   411→      try {
   412→        queryClient.setQueryData(['resources', 'style-reference'], (prev: Resource[] | undefined) => {
   413→          const prevResources = prev || [];
   414→          return [...prevResources, resource];
   415→        });
   416→
   417→        queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.settings.tool('project-image-settings', selectedProjectId, undefined), (prev: unknown) =>
   418→          updateSettingsCache<ProjectImageSettings>(prev, (prevSettings) => ({
   419→            references: [...(prevSettings?.references || []), newPointer],
   420→            selectedReferenceIdByShot: {
   421→              ...(prevSettings?.selectedReferenceIdByShot || {}),
   422→              [effectiveShotId]: newPointer.id
   423→            }
   424→          }))
   425→        );
   426→      } catch (e) {
   427→      }
   428→
   429→      // Read from cache after optimistic update
   430→      const currentData = extractSettingsFromCache<ProjectImageSettings>(
   431→        queryClient.getQueryData(queryKeys.settings.tool('project-image-settings', selectedProjectId, undefined))
   432→      ) || {};
   433→
   434→      await updateProjectImageSettings('project', {
   435→        references: currentData?.references || [],
   436→        selectedReferenceIdByShot: currentData?.selectedReferenceIdByShot || {}
   437→      });
   438→
   439→      markAsInteracted();
   440→      setStyleReferenceOverride(originalUploadedUrl);
   441→
   442→    } catch (error) {
   443→      handleError(error, { context: 'useReferenceManagement.handleStyleReferenceUpload', toastTitle: 'Failed to upload reference image' });
   444→    } finally {
   445→      setIsUploadingStyleReference(false);
   446→    }
   447→  }, [
   448→    effectiveShotId,
   449→    updateProjectImageSettings,
   450→    markAsInteracted,
   451→    selectedProjectId,
   452→    hydratedReferences,
   453→    queryClient,
   454→    createStyleReference,
   455→    isLoadingProjectSettings,
   456→    privacyDefaults,
   457→  ]);
   458→
   459→  // ============================================================================
   460→  // Handler: Select Existing Resource
   461→  // ============================================================================
   462→
   463→  const handleResourceSelect = useCallback(async (resource: Resource) => {
   464→    if (isLoadingProjectSettings) {
   465→      toast.error('Please wait for settings to load');
   466→      return;
   467→    }
   468→
   469→    try {
   470→      // Check if we already have this resource linked
   471→      const existingPointer = referencePointers.find(ptr => ptr.resourceId === resource.id);
   472→
   473→      if (existingPointer) {
   474→
   475→        const optimisticUpdate = {
   476→          ...selectedReferenceIdByShot,
   477→          [effectiveShotId]: existingPointer.id
   478→        };
   479→
   480→        try {
   481→          queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.settings.tool('project-image-settings', selectedProjectId, undefined), (prev: unknown) =>
   482→            updateSettingsCache<ProjectImageSettings>(prev, { selectedReferenceIdByShot: optimisticUpdate })
   483→          );
   484→        } catch (e) {
   485→        }
   486→
   487→        await updateProjectImageSettings('project', {
   488→          selectedReferenceIdByShot: optimisticUpdate
   489→        });
   490→
   491→        markAsInteracted();
   492→        return;
   493→      }
   494→
   495→      // Create lightweight pointer to existing resource
   496→      const modeDefaults = referenceMode === 'custom'
   497→        ? { styleReferenceStrength, subjectStrength, inThisScene, inThisSceneStrength }
   498→        : getReferenceModeDefaults(referenceMode, isLocalGenerationEnabled);
   499→
   500→      const newPointer: ReferenceImage = {
   501→        id: nanoid(),
   502→        resourceId: resource.id,
   503→        subjectDescription: '',
   504→        styleBoostTerms: '',
   505→        referenceMode: referenceMode,
   506→        createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
   507→        ...modeDefaults,
   508→      };
   509→
   510→      // Optimistic UI update
   511→      try {
   512→        queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.settings.tool('project-image-settings', selectedProjectId, undefined), (prev: unknown) =>
   513→          updateSettingsCache<ProjectImageSettings>(prev, (prevSettings) => ({
   514→            references: [...(prevSettings?.references || []), newPointer],
   515→            selectedReferenceIdByShot: {
   516→              ...(prevSettings?.selectedReferenceIdByShot || {}),
   517→              [effectiveShotId]: newPointer.id
   518→            }
   519→          }))
   520→        );
   521→      } catch (e) {
   522→        handleError(e, { context: 'useReferenceManagement.handleResourceSelect.optimisticUpdate', showToast: false });
   523→      }
   524→
   525→      const currentData = extractSettingsFromCache<ProjectImageSettings>(
   526→        queryClient.getQueryData(queryKeys.settings.tool('project-image-settings', selectedProjectId, undefined))
   527→      ) || {};
   528→
   529→      await updateProjectImageSettings('project', {
   530→        references: currentData?.references || [],
   531→        selectedReferenceIdByShot: currentData?.selectedReferenceIdByShot || {}
   532→      });
   533→
   534→      markAsInteracted();
   535→    } catch (error) {
   536→      handleError(error, { context: 'useReferenceManagement.handleResourceSelect', toastTitle: 'Failed to add reference' });
   537→    }
   538→  }, [
   539→    effectiveShotId,
   540→    updateProjectImageSettings,
   541→    queryClient,
   542→    selectedProjectId,
   543→    markAsInteracted,
   544→    referencePointers,
   545→    selectedReferenceIdByShot,
   546→    referenceMode,
   547→    styleReferenceStrength,
   548→    subjectStrength,
   549→    inThisScene,
   550→    inThisSceneStrength,
   551→    isLoadingProjectSettings,
   552→    isLocalGenerationEnabled,
   553→  ]);
   554→
   555→  // ============================================================================
   556→  // Handler: Select Reference for Current Shot
   557→  // ============================================================================
   558→
   559→  const handleSelectReference = useCallback(async (referenceId: string) => {
   560→
   561→    // Also update shot-level settings for inheritance
   562→    if (associatedShotId && shotPromptSettings) {
   563→      shotPromptSettings.updateField('selectedReferenceId', referenceId);
   564→    }
   565→
   566→    // Optimistic UI update for project-level per-shot mapping
   567→    const optimisticUpdate = {
   568→      ...selectedReferenceIdByShot,
   569→      [effectiveShotId]: referenceId
   570→    };
   571→
   572→    try {
   573→      queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.settings.tool('project-image-settings', selectedProjectId, undefined), (prev: unknown) =>
   574→        updateSettingsCache<ProjectImageSettings>(prev, { selectedReferenceIdByShot: optimisticUpdate })
   575→      );
   576→    } catch (e) {
   577→    }
   578→
   579→    await updateProjectImageSettings('project', {
   580→      selectedReferenceIdByShot: optimisticUpdate
   581→    });
   582→    markAsInteracted();
   583→  }, [effectiveShotId, selectedReferenceIdByShot, updateProjectImageSettings, markAsInteracted, queryClient, selectedProjectId, associatedShotId, shotPromptSettings]);
   584→
   585→  // ============================================================================
   586→  // Handler: Delete Reference
   587→  // ============================================================================
   588→
   589→  const handleDeleteReference = useCallback(async (referenceId: string) => {
   590→
   591→    const hydratedRef = hydratedReferences.find(r => r.id === referenceId);
   592→    if (!hydratedRef) {
   593→      console.error('[useReferenceManagement] Could not find reference:', referenceId);
   594→      return;
   595→    }
   596→
   597→    // Delete the resource from resources table
   598→    try {
   599→      await deleteStyleReference.mutateAsync({
   600→        id: hydratedRef.resourceId,
   601→        type: 'style-reference',
   602→      });
   603→    } catch (error) {
   604→      handleError(error, { context: 'useReferenceManagement.handleDeleteReference', toastTitle: 'Failed to delete reference' });
   605→      return;
   606→    }
   607→
   608→    // Remove pointer from settings
   609→    const filteredPointers = referencePointers.filter(ref => ref.id !== referenceId);
   610→
   611→    // Update all shot selections that had this reference selected
   612→    const updatedSelections = { ...selectedReferenceIdByShot };
   613→    Object.keys(updatedSelections).forEach(shotId => {
   614→      if (updatedSelections[shotId] === referenceId) {
   615→        updatedSelections[shotId] = filteredPointers[0]?.id ?? null;
   616→      }
   617→    });
   618→
   619→    // Optimistic UI update
   620→    try {
   621→      queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.settings.tool('project-image-settings', selectedProjectId, undefined), (prev: unknown) =>
   622→        updateSettingsCache<ProjectImageSettings>(prev, {
   623→          references: filteredPointers,
   624→          selectedReferenceIdByShot: updatedSelections
   625→        })
   626→      );
   627→    } catch (e) {
   628→    }
   629→
   630→    await updateProjectImageSettings('project', {
   631→      references: filteredPointers,
   632→      selectedReferenceIdByShot: updatedSelections
   633→    });
   634→
   635→    markAsInteracted();
   636→  }, [hydratedReferences, referencePointers, selectedReferenceIdByShot, deleteStyleReference, updateProjectImageSettings, markAsInteracted, queryClient, selectedProjectId]);
   637→
   638→  // ============================================================================
   639→  // Handler: Update Reference Name
   640→  // ============================================================================
   641→
   642→  const handleUpdateReferenceName = useCallback(async (referenceId: string, name: string) => {
   643→
   644→    // Find the hydrated reference to get the resourceId
   645→    const hydratedRef = hydratedReferences.find(r => r.id === referenceId);
   646→    if (!hydratedRef) {
   647→      console.error('[useReferenceManagement] Could not find reference:', referenceId);
   648→      return;
   649→    }
   650→
   651→    // Name is stored on the Resource, not the pointer - update via updateStyleReference
   652→    try {
   653→      const updatedMetadata: StyleReferenceMetadata = {
   654→        name,
   655→        styleReferenceImage: hydratedRef.styleReferenceImage,
   656→        styleReferenceImageOriginal: hydratedRef.styleReferenceImageOriginal,
   657→        thumbnailUrl: hydratedRef.thumbnailUrl,
   658→        styleReferenceStrength: hydratedRef.styleReferenceStrength,
   659→        subjectStrength: hydratedRef.subjectStrength,
   660→        subjectDescription: hydratedRef.subjectDescription,
   661→        inThisScene: hydratedRef.inThisScene,
   662→        inThisSceneStrength: hydratedRef.inThisSceneStrength,
   663→        referenceMode: hydratedRef.referenceMode,
   664→        styleBoostTerms: hydratedRef.styleBoostTerms,
   665→        created_by: { is_you: true },
   666→        is_public: hydratedRef.isPublic,
   667→        createdAt: hydratedRef.createdAt,
   668→        updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
   669→      };
   670→
   671→      await updateStyleReference.mutateAsync({
   672→        id: hydratedRef.resourceId,
   673→        type: 'style-reference',
   674→        metadata: updatedMetadata,
   675→      });
   676→
   677→    } catch (error) {
   678→      handleError(error, { context: 'useReferenceManagement.handleUpdateReferenceName', toastTitle: 'Failed to update name' });
   679→    }
   680→  }, [hydratedReferences, updateStyleReference]);
   681→
   682→  // ============================================================================
   683→  // Handler: Toggle Visibility
   684→  // ============================================================================
   685→
   686→  const handleToggleVisibility = useCallback(async (resourceId: string, currentIsPublic: boolean) => {
   687→
   688→    const hydratedRef = hydratedReferences.find(r => r.resourceId === resourceId);
   689→    if (!hydratedRef) {
   690→      console.error('[useReferenceManagement] Could not find reference with resourceId:', resourceId);
   691→      return;
   692→    }
   693→
   694→    try {
   695→      const updatedMetadata: StyleReferenceMetadata = {
   696→        name: hydratedRef.name,
   697→        styleReferenceImage: hydratedRef.styleReferenceImage,
   698→        styleReferenceImageOriginal: hydratedRef.styleReferenceImageOriginal,
   699→        thumbnailUrl: hydratedRef.thumbnailUrl,
   700→        styleReferenceStrength: hydratedRef.styleReferenceStrength,
   701→        subjectStrength: hydratedRef.subjectStrength,
   702→        subjectDescription: hydratedRef.subjectDescription,
   703→        inThisScene: hydratedRef.inThisScene,
   704→        inThisSceneStrength: hydratedRef.inThisSceneStrength,
   705→        referenceMode: hydratedRef.referenceMode,
   706→        styleBoostTerms: hydratedRef.styleBoostTerms,
   707→        created_by: { is_you: true },
   708→        is_public: !currentIsPublic,
   709→        createdAt: hydratedRef.createdAt,
   710→        updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
   711→      };
   712→
   713→      await updateStyleReference.mutateAsync({
   714→        id: resourceId,
   715→        type: 'style-reference',
   716→        metadata: updatedMetadata,
   717→      });
   718→
   719→    } catch (error) {
   720→      handleError(error, { context: 'useReferenceManagement.handleToggleVisibility', toastTitle: 'Failed to update visibility' });
   721→    }
   722→  }, [hydratedReferences, updateStyleReference]);
   723→
   724→  // ============================================================================
   725→  // Handler: Remove Style Reference (legacy)
   726→  // ============================================================================
   727→
   728→  const handleRemoveStyleReference = useCallback(async () => {
   729→    if (!selectedReferenceId) return;
   730→    await handleDeleteReference(selectedReferenceId);
   731→  }, [selectedReferenceId, handleDeleteReference]);
   732→
   733→  // ============================================================================
   734→  // Strength and Settings Handlers
   735→  // ============================================================================
   736→
   737→  const handleStyleStrengthChange = useCallback(async (value: number) => {
   738→    if (!selectedReferenceId) return;
   739→    setStyleReferenceStrength(value);
   740→    await handleUpdateReference(selectedReferenceId, { styleReferenceStrength: value });
   741→  }, [selectedReferenceId, handleUpdateReference]);
   742→
   743→  const handleSubjectStrengthChange = useCallback(async (value: number) => {
   744→    if (!selectedReferenceId) return;
   745→    setSubjectStrength(value);
   746→    await handleUpdateReference(selectedReferenceId, { subjectStrength: value });
   747→  }, [selectedReferenceId, handleUpdateReference]);
   748→
   749→  const handleSubjectDescriptionChange = useCallback(async (value: string) => {
   750→    if (!selectedReferenceId) return;
   751→    setSubjectDescription(value);
   752→    await handleUpdateReference(selectedReferenceId, { subjectDescription: value });
   753→  }, [selectedReferenceId, handleUpdateReference]);
   754→
   755→  const handleSubjectDescriptionFocus = useCallback(() => {
   756→    setIsEditingSubjectDescription(true);
   757→  }, []);
   758→
   759→  const handleSubjectDescriptionBlur = useCallback(() => {
   760→    setIsEditingSubjectDescription(false);
   761→  }, []);
   762→
   763→  const handleInThisSceneChange = useCallback(async (value: boolean) => {
   764→    if (!selectedReferenceId) return;
   765→    setInThisScene(value);
   766→    await handleUpdateReference(selectedReferenceId, { inThisScene: value });
   767→  }, [selectedReferenceId, handleUpdateReference]);
   768→
   769→  const handleInThisSceneStrengthChange = useCallback(async (value: number) => {
   770→    if (!selectedReferenceId) return;
   771→    setInThisSceneStrength(value);
   772→    await handleUpdateReference(selectedReferenceId, { inThisSceneStrength: value });
   773→  }, [selectedReferenceId, handleUpdateReference]);
   774→
   775→  const handleStyleBoostTermsChange = useCallback(async (value: string) => {
   776→    if (!selectedReferenceId) return;
   777→    setStyleBoostTerms(value);
   778→    await handleUpdateReference(selectedReferenceId, { styleBoostTerms: value });
   779→  }, [selectedReferenceId, handleUpdateReference]);
   780→
   781→  // ============================================================================
   782→  // Handler: Reference Mode Change
   783→  // ============================================================================
   784→
   785→  const handleReferenceModeChange = useCallback(async (mode: ReferenceMode) => {
   786→    if (!selectedReferenceId) return;
   787→
   788→    // Get defaults for this mode and generation environment
   789→    const defaults = getReferenceModeDefaults(mode, isLocalGenerationEnabled);
   790→
   791→    // Build update object with mode AND auto-set strength values
   792→    const updates: Partial<ReferenceImage> = {
   793→      referenceMode: mode,
   794→      ...defaults,
   795→    };
   796→
   797→    // For custom mode, only apply defaults if current strengths are too low
   798→    if (mode === 'custom') {
   799→      const currentTotal = styleReferenceStrength + subjectStrength;
   800→      if (currentTotal >= 0.5) {
   801→        delete updates.styleReferenceStrength;
   802→        delete updates.subjectStrength;
   803→        delete updates.inThisScene;
   804→        delete updates.inThisSceneStrength;
   805→      }
   806→    }
   807→
   808→    // Optimistic local updates
   809→    pendingReferenceModeUpdate.current = mode;
   810→    setReferenceMode(mode);
   811→    if (updates.styleReferenceStrength !== undefined) {
   812→      setStyleReferenceStrength(updates.styleReferenceStrength);
   813→    }
   814→    if (updates.subjectStrength !== undefined) {
   815→      setSubjectStrength(updates.subjectStrength);
   816→    }
   817→    if (updates.inThisScene !== undefined) {
   818→      setInThisScene(updates.inThisScene);
   819→    }
   820→    if (updates.inThisSceneStrength !== undefined) {
   821→      setInThisSceneStrength(updates.inThisSceneStrength);
   822→    }
   823→
   824→    // Set denoise to 0.5 for Subject and Scene modes
   825→    if ((mode === 'subject' || mode === 'scene') && setHiresFixConfig) {
   826→      setHiresFixConfig((prev) => ({ ...prev, hires_denoise: 0.5 }));
   827→    }
   828→
   829→    await handleUpdateReference(selectedReferenceId, updates);
   830→  }, [selectedReferenceId, handleUpdateReference, styleReferenceStrength, subjectStrength, isLocalGenerationEnabled, setHiresFixConfig]);
   831→
   832→  // ============================================================================
   833→  // Return
   834→  // ============================================================================
   835→
   836→  return {
   837→    // Local UI state
   838→    styleReferenceStrength,
   839→    subjectStrength,
   840→    subjectDescription,
   841→    isEditingSubjectDescription,
   842→    inThisScene,
   843→    inThisSceneStrength,
   844→    referenceMode,
   845→    styleBoostTerms,
   846→    isUploadingStyleReference,
   847→    styleReferenceOverride,
   848→
   849→    // Display computed values
   850→    styleReferenceImageDisplay,
   851→    styleReferenceImageGeneration,
   852→
   853→    // Handlers
   854→    handleStyleReferenceUpload,
   855→    handleResourceSelect,
   856→    handleSelectReference,
   857→    handleDeleteReference,
   858→    handleUpdateReference,
   859→    handleUpdateReferenceName,
   860→    handleToggleVisibility,
   861→    handleRemoveStyleReference,
   862→    handleStyleStrengthChange,
   863→    handleSubjectStrengthChange,
   864→    handleSubjectDescriptionChange,
   865→    handleSubjectDescriptionFocus,
   866→    handleSubjectDescriptionBlur,
   867→    handleInThisSceneChange,
   868→    handleInThisSceneStrengthChange,
   869→    handleStyleBoostTermsChange,
   870→    handleReferenceModeChange,
   871→
   872→    // State setters
   873→    setStyleReferenceStrength,
   874→    setSubjectStrength,
   875→    setSubjectDescription,
   876→    setInThisScene,
   877→    setInThisSceneStrength,
   878→    setReferenceMode,
   879→    setStyleBoostTerms,

    12→import { useListShots } from "@/shared/hooks/useShots";
    13→import { useShotCreation } from "@/shared/hooks/useShotCreation";
    14→import CreateShotModal from "@/shared/components/CreateShotModal";
    15→import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
    16→import { queryKeys } from '@/shared/lib/queryKeys';
    17→import { useShotNavigation } from "@/shared/hooks/useShotNavigation";
    18→import { BatchImageGenerationTaskParams } from "@/shared/lib/tasks/imageGeneration";
    19→import { ASPECT_RATIO_TO_RESOLUTION } from "@/shared/lib/aspectRatios";
    20→import { useAIInteractionService } from '@/shared/hooks/useAIInteractionService';
    21→import { useSubmitButtonState } from '@/shared/hooks/useSubmitButtonState';
    22→import { useHydratedReferences } from '@/shared/hooks/useHydratedReferences';
    23→
    24→// Import extracted components
    25→import { PromptsSection } from "./components/PromptsSection";
    26→import { ShotSelector } from "./components/ShotSelector";
    27→import { ModelSection } from "./components/ModelSection";
    28→import { GenerateControls } from "./components/GenerateControls";
    29→import { GenerationSettingsSection } from "./components/GenerationSettingsSection";
    30→import { ChunkLoadErrorBoundary } from "@/shared/components/ChunkLoadErrorBoundary";
    31→
    32→// Import extracted hooks
    33→import {
    34→  useGenerationSource,
    35→  usePromptManagement,
    36→  useReferenceManagement,
    37→  useFormSubmission,
    38→  useLegacyMigrations,
    39→  useReferenceSelection,
    40→  useLoraHandlers,
    41→} from "./hooks";
    42→
    43→// Import state management
    44→import { useFormUIState } from "./state";
    45→
    46→// Import context
    47→import {
    48→  ImageGenerationFormProvider,
    49→  useContextValue,
    50→  FormCoreState,
    51→  FormPromptState,
    52→  FormPromptHandlers,
    53→  FormReferenceState,
    54→  FormReferenceHandlers,
    55→  FormLoraState,
    56→  FormLoraHandlers,
    57→} from "./ImageGenerationFormContext";
    58→
    59→// Import types
    60→import {
    61→  PromptEntry,
    62→  PersistedFormSettings,
    63→  ProjectImageSettings,
    64→  PromptMode,
    65→  ImageGenShotSettings,
    66→  HiresFixConfig,
    67→  DEFAULT_HIRES_FIX_CONFIG,
    68→  TextToImageModel,
    69→  LoraCategory,
    70→  getLoraTypeForModel,
    71→  getLoraCategoryForModel,
    72→  BY_REFERENCE_LORA_TYPE,
    73→} from "./types";
    74→
    75→// Lazy load modals to improve initial bundle size and performance
    76→const LazyLoraSelectorModal = React.lazy(() => 
    77→  import("@/shared/components/LoraSelectorModal").then(module => ({ 
    78→    default: module.LoraSelectorModal 
    79→  }))
    80→);
    81→
    82→const LazyPromptEditorModal = React.lazy(() =>
    83→  import("../PromptEditorModal")
    84→);
    85→
    86→interface ImageGenerationFormProps {
    87→  onGenerate: (params: BatchImageGenerationTaskParams) => Promise<string[]> | string[] | void;
    88→  openaiApiKey?: string;
    89→  /**
    90→   * Called when the associated shot selection changes in the form
    91→   */
    92→  onShotChange?: (shotId: string | null) => void;
    93→  /**
    94→   * When true, the generate controls will be rendered with sticky positioning
    95→   * at the bottom of the scroll container (for modal contexts)
    96→   */
    97→  stickyFooter?: boolean;
    98→  /**
    99→   * When provided with stickyFooter, the footer will be portaled to this element
   100→   * (by ID) so it renders outside the scroll container
   101→   */
   102→  footerPortalId?: string;
   103→  /**
   104→   * Pre-select a specific shot when the form mounts. This takes precedence over
   105→   * persisted settings on initial render only.
   106→   */
   107→  initialShotId?: string | null;
   108→}
   109→
   110→export const ImageGenerationForm: React.FC<ImageGenerationFormProps> = ({
   111→  onGenerate,
   112→  openaiApiKey,
   113→  onShotChange,
   114→  stickyFooter = false,
   115→  footerPortalId,
   116→  initialShotId,
   117→}) => {
   118→  // ============================================================================
   119→  // UI State (reducer for modal states, session tracking)
   120→  // ============================================================================
   121→  const { uiState, uiActions } = useFormUIState();
   122→
   123→  const [imagesPerPrompt, setImagesPerPrompt] = useState(8); // Default to 8 for automated mode
   124→  const [promptMultiplier, setPromptMultiplier] = useState(1); // How many images per prompt in automated mode
   125→  const [hiresFixConfigRaw, setHiresFixConfig] = useState<Partial<HiresFixConfig>>({});
   126→  // Always merge with defaults to handle incomplete persisted data
   127→  // Also migrate legacy camelCase field names to snake_case
   128→  const hiresFixConfig = useMemo<HiresFixConfig>(() => {
   129→    const raw = hiresFixConfigRaw as Record<string, unknown>;
   130→    // Migrate legacy camelCase fields if present
   131→    const migrated: Partial<HiresFixConfig> = {
   132→      ...hiresFixConfigRaw,
   133→      // Map old camelCase to new snake_case (prefer new names if both exist)
   134→      base_steps: (raw.base_steps as number) ?? (raw.baseSteps as number),
   135→      hires_scale: (raw.hires_scale as number) ?? (raw.hiresScale as number),
   136→      hires_steps: (raw.hires_steps as number) ?? (raw.hiresSteps as number),
   137→      hires_denoise: (raw.hires_denoise as number) ?? (raw.hiresDenoise as number),
   138→      // Migrate from old single lightning_lora_strength to phase_1 (phase_2 defaults to 0)
   139→      lightning_lora_strength_phase_1: (raw.lightning_lora_strength_phase_1 as number)
   140→        ?? (raw.lightning_lora_strength as number)
   141→        ?? (raw.lightningLoraStrength as number),
   142→      lightning_lora_strength_phase_2: (raw.lightning_lora_strength_phase_2 as number),
   143→    };
   144→    return {
   145→      ...DEFAULT_HIRES_FIX_CONFIG,
   146→      ...migrated,
   147→    };
   148→  }, [hiresFixConfigRaw]);
   149→  // Associated shot for image generation
   150→  const [associatedShotId, setAssociatedShotId] = useState<string | null>(null);
   151→  
   152→  const { selectedProjectId, projects } = useProject();
   153→  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
   154→
   155→  // Get current task count for baseline tracking
   156→
   157→  // Derive project aspect ratio and resolution for GenerationSettingsSection
   158→  const { projectAspectRatio, projectResolution } = useMemo(() => {
   159→    const currentProject = projects.find(project => project.id === selectedProjectId);
   160→    const aspectRatio = currentProject?.aspectRatio ?? '16:9';
   161→    const resolution = ASPECT_RATIO_TO_RESOLUTION[aspectRatio] ?? '902x508';
   162→    return { projectAspectRatio: aspectRatio, projectResolution: resolution };
   163→  }, [projects, selectedProjectId]);
   164→  
   165→  // Access user's generation settings to detect local generation
   166→  const { value: generationMethods } = useUserUIState('generationMethods', { onComputer: true, inCloud: true });
   167→  
   168→  // Privacy defaults for new resources
   169→  const { value: privacyDefaults } = useUserUIState('privacyDefaults', { resourcesPublic: true, generationsPublic: false });
   170→  
   171→  const isLocalGenerationEnabled = generationMethods.onComputer && !generationMethods.inCloud;
   172→  
   173→  // Project-level settings for model and style reference (shared across tools)
   174→  const {
   175→    settings: projectImageSettings,
   176→    update: updateProjectImageSettings,
   177→    isUpdating: isSavingProjectSettings,
   178→    isLoading: isLoadingProjectSettings
   179→  } = useToolSettings<ProjectImageSettings>('project-image-settings', {
   180→    projectId: selectedProjectId,
   181→    enabled: !!selectedProjectId
   182→  });
   183→
   184→  // Get the effective shot ID for storage (use 'none' for null)
   185→  const effectiveShotId = associatedShotId || 'none';
   186→  
   187→  // Get reference pointers array and selected reference for current shot
   188→  const cachedProjectSettings = selectedProjectId
   189→    ? extractSettingsFromCache<ProjectImageSettings>(
   190→        queryClient.getQueryData(queryKeys.settings.tool('project-image-settings', selectedProjectId, undefined))
   191→      )
   192→    : undefined;
   193→
   194→  const referencePointers = projectImageSettings?.references ?? cachedProjectSettings?.references ?? [];
   195→  // Reference count is simply the number of pointers - no complex tracking needed
   196→  // Skeleton count is managed by comparing referencePointers.length vs hydratedReferences.length
   197→  const referenceCount = referencePointers.length;
   198→  // Use cache fallback for selection too, so selection is stable during loading
   199→  const selectedReferenceIdByShot = projectImageSettings?.selectedReferenceIdByShot ?? cachedProjectSettings?.selectedReferenceIdByShot ?? {};
   200→  const selectedReferenceId = selectedReferenceIdByShot[effectiveShotId] ?? null;
   201→
   202→  // Hydrate references with data from resources table
   203→  const { hydratedReferences, isLoading: isLoadingReferences, hasLegacyReferences } = useHydratedReferences(referencePointers);
   204→
   205→  // ============================================================================
   206→  // Reference Selection (extracted hook)
   207→  // ============================================================================
   208→  const {
   209→    displayedReferenceId,
   210→    selectedReference,
   211→    isReferenceDataLoading,
   212→  } = useReferenceSelection({
   213→    effectiveShotId,
   214→    referenceCount,
   215→    selectedReferenceId,
   216→    hydratedReferences,
   217→    isLoadingProjectSettings,
   218→    isLoadingReferences,
   219→  });
   220→
   221→  // For backward compatibility with single reference - used in legacy migration
   222→  const rawStyleReferenceImage = selectedReference?.styleReferenceImage || projectImageSettings?.styleReferenceImage || null;
   223→
   224→  // ============================================================================
   225→  // Legacy Migrations (extracted hook)
   226→  // ============================================================================
   227→  useLegacyMigrations({
   228→    selectedProjectId,
   229→    effectiveShotId,
   230→    referencePointers,
   231→    hydratedReferences,
   232→    hasLegacyReferences,
   233→    rawStyleReferenceImage,
   234→    isLoadingReferences,
   235→    selectedReferenceIdByShot,
   236→    projectImageSettings,
   237→    updateProjectImageSettings,
   238→    privacyDefaults,
   239→  });
   240→  
   241→  // Mark that we've visited this page in the session
   242→  React.useEffect(() => {
   243→    try {
   244→      if (!uiState.hasVisitedImageGeneration && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
   245→        window.sessionStorage.setItem('hasVisitedImageGeneration', 'true');
   246→        uiActions.setHasVisited(true);
   247→      }
   248→    } catch { /* Ignore sessionStorage errors */ }
   249→  }, [uiState.hasVisitedImageGeneration, uiActions]);
   250→
   251→  // Text to prepend/append to every prompt
   252→  const [beforeEachPromptText, setBeforeEachPromptText] = useState("");
   253→  const [afterEachPromptText, setAfterEachPromptText] = useState("");
   254→  // Prompt mode: automated vs managed (default to automated)
   255→  const [promptMode, setPromptMode] = useState<PromptMode>('automated');
   256→  // Local state for prompts when no shot is selected
   257→  const [noShotPrompts, setNoShotPrompts] = useState<PromptEntry[]>([]);
   258→  const [noShotMasterPrompt, setNoShotMasterPrompt] = useState('');
   259→
   260→  // Optimistic button state for immediate feedback: idle → submitting → success → idle
   261→  const automatedSubmitButton = useSubmitButtonState();
   262→
   263→  // Removed unused currentShotId that was causing unnecessary re-renders
   264→  const { data: shots } = useListShots(selectedProjectId);
   265→  const { createShot, isCreating: isCreatingShot } = useShotCreation();
   266→  const { navigateToShot } = useShotNavigation();
   267→  
   268→  // Define generatePromptId before using it in hooks
   269→  const promptIdCounter = useRef(1);
   270→  const generatePromptId = useCallback(() => `prompt-${promptIdCounter.current++}`, []);
   271→  
   272→  // AI interaction service for automated prompt generation
   273→  const { generatePrompts: aiGeneratePrompts } = useAIInteractionService({
   274→    apiKey: openaiApiKey,
   275→    generatePromptId,
   276→  });
   277→
   278→  // Debug logging consolidated - enable via VITE_DEBUG_IMAGE_GEN=true
   279→  const DEBUG_IMAGE_GEN = import.meta.env.VITE_DEBUG_IMAGE_GEN === 'true';
   280→
   281→  // Fetch public LoRAs from all users
   282→  const { data: availableLoras } = usePublicLoras();
   283→
   284→  // LoRA management using the modularized hook with new generalized approach
   285→  const loraManager = useLoraManager(availableLoras, {
   286→    projectId: selectedProjectId,
   287→    persistenceScope: 'project', // Use new persistence scope
   288→    enableProjectPersistence: true,
   289→    persistenceKey: 'project-loras', // Standardized key shared across all tools
   290→    enableTriggerWords: true,
   291→    onPromptUpdate: setAfterEachPromptText,
   292→    currentPrompt: afterEachPromptText,
   293→    disableAutoLoad: true, // Disable auto-load since we handle our own default logic
   294→  });
   295→
   296→  // Default settings for shot prompts - recomputed when shot changes to pick up fresh localStorage
   297→  // Inheritance chain: localStorage (last edited shot) → project-level settings → hardcoded defaults
   298→  // Reference selection defaults to most-recent reference (handled in auto-select effect)
   299→  // Note: beforeEachPromptText/afterEachPromptText are persisted per-shot but NOT inherited (default empty)
   300→  const shotPromptDefaults = useMemo<ImageGenShotSettings>(() => {
   301→    // Try to load last active shot settings for inheritance
   302→    try {
   303→      const stored = localStorage.getItem('image-gen-last-active-shot-settings');
   304→      if (stored) {
   305→        const parsed = JSON.parse(stored);
   306→        return {
   307→          prompts: [],
   308→          masterPrompt: parsed.masterPrompt || '',
   309→          promptMode: parsed.promptMode || 'automated',
   310→          // IMPORTANT: Do NOT default this to null.
   311→          // null is treated as an explicit override and would suppress the project-level
   312→          // per-shot selection mapping (selectedReferenceIdByShot), leading to "no selection"
   313→          // and jitter/jumps when the user clicks.
   314→          // Leave undefined so we can fall back to project-level mapping until the user picks one.
   315→        };
   316→      }
   317→    } catch {
   318→      // Ignore localStorage errors
   319→    }
   320→    // Fall back to project-level settings if localStorage is empty
   321→    return { 
   322→      prompts: [], 
   323→      masterPrompt: noShotMasterPrompt || '',
   324→      promptMode: promptMode || 'automated',
   325→    };
   326→  // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps -- associatedShotId triggers recompute to pick up fresh localStorage
   327→  }, [associatedShotId, noShotMasterPrompt, promptMode]);
   328→
   329→  // Shot-specific prompts using per-shot storage
   330→  const shotPromptSettings = useAutoSaveSettings<ImageGenShotSettings>({
   331→    toolId: 'image-gen-prompts',
   332→    shotId: associatedShotId,
   333→    projectId: selectedProjectId,
   334→    scope: 'shot',
   335→    defaults: shotPromptDefaults,
   336→    enabled: !!associatedShotId,
   337→  });
   338→
   339→  // Project-level settings (NOT shot-specific)
   340→  // Note: promptMode here is the fallback when no shot is selected
   341→  // beforeEachPromptText/afterEachPromptText persist but default to empty (not inherited from other shots)
   342→  const { ready, isSaving, markAsInteracted } = usePersistentToolState<PersistedFormSettings>(
   343→    'image-generation',
   344→    { projectId: selectedProjectId },
   345→    {
   346→      imagesPerPrompt: [imagesPerPrompt, setImagesPerPrompt],
   347→      selectedLoras: [loraManager.selectedLoras, loraManager.setSelectedLoras],
   348→      beforeEachPromptText: [beforeEachPromptText, setBeforeEachPromptText],
   349→      afterEachPromptText: [afterEachPromptText, setAfterEachPromptText],
   350→      associatedShotId: [associatedShotId, setAssociatedShotId],
   351→      promptMode: [promptMode, setPromptMode],
   352→      prompts: [noShotPrompts, setNoShotPrompts], // Persist no-shot prompts
   353→      masterPrompt: [noShotMasterPrompt, setNoShotMasterPrompt], // Persist no-shot master prompt
   354→      hiresFixConfig: [hiresFixConfig, setHiresFixConfig],
   355→    }
   356→  );
   357→
   358→  // ============================================================================
   359→  // Generation Source & Model Selection (extracted hook)
   360→  // ============================================================================
   361→  const {
   362→    generationSource,
   363→    selectedTextModel,
   364→    modelOverride,
   365→    generationSourceRef,
   366→    selectedTextModelRef,
   367→    handleGenerationSourceChange,
   368→    handleTextModelChange,
   369→    setGenerationSource,
   370→    setSelectedTextModel,
   371→    setModelOverride,
   372→  } = useGenerationSource({
   373→    selectedProjectId,
   374→    projectImageSettings,
   375→    isLoadingProjectSettings,
   376→    updateProjectImageSettings,
   377→    markAsInteracted,
   378→    loraManager: {
   379→      selectedLoras: loraManager.selectedLoras,
   380→      setSelectedLoras: loraManager.setSelectedLoras,
   381→    },
   382→    setHiresFixConfig,
   383→    queryClient,
   384→  });
   385→
   386→  // Clear model override once server settings reflect the change
   387→  useEffect(() => {
   388→    if (modelOverride && projectImageSettings?.selectedModel === modelOverride) {
   389→      setModelOverride(undefined);
   390→    }
   391→  }, [projectImageSettings?.selectedModel, modelOverride, isSavingProjectSettings, setModelOverride]);
   392→
   393→  // Initialize generationSource and selectedTextModel from project settings
   394→  // (LORA initialization is done separately after loraManager is created)
   395→  const hasInitializedGenerationSource = useRef(false);
   396→  const initializedTextModelRef = useRef<TextToImageModel | null>(null);
   397→  useEffect(() => {
   398→    if (isLoadingProjectSettings) return;
   399→    if (hasInitializedGenerationSource.current) return;
   400→    if (!projectImageSettings) return;
   401→
   402→    if (projectImageSettings.generationSource) {
   403→      setGenerationSource(projectImageSettings.generationSource);
   404→    }
   405→    const textModel = projectImageSettings.selectedTextModel || 'qwen-image';
   406→    if (projectImageSettings.selectedTextModel) {
   407→      setSelectedTextModel(projectImageSettings.selectedTextModel);
   408→    }
   409→    // Store for LORA initialization (done in separate effect after loraManager is created)
   410→    initializedTextModelRef.current = textModel;
   411→    hasInitializedGenerationSource.current = true;
   412→  }, [projectImageSettings, selectedProjectId, isLoadingProjectSettings, setGenerationSource, setSelectedTextModel]);
   413→
   414→  // Initialize LORAs from per-category storage (runs after loraManager is created)
   415→  // Categories: 'qwen' (all Qwen models + by-reference) and 'z-image'
   416→  const hasInitializedLoras = useRef(false);
   417→  useEffect(() => {
   418→    if (isLoadingProjectSettings) return;
   419→    if (hasInitializedLoras.current) return;
   420→
   421→    // Determine current category based on generation source and model
   422→    const textModel = initializedTextModelRef.current || selectedTextModel;
   423→    const currentSource = projectImageSettings?.generationSource || generationSource;
   424→    // by-reference always uses 'qwen' category
   425→    const category: LoraCategory = currentSource === 'by-reference' ? 'qwen' : getLoraCategoryForModel(textModel);
   426→
   427→    // Try new category-based storage first, fall back to old per-model storage for migration
   428→    let categoryLoras: ActiveLora[] = [];
   429→    if (projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByCategory) {
   430→      categoryLoras = projectImageSettings.selectedLorasByCategory[category] ?? [];
   431→    } else if (projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByTextModel) {
   432→      // Migration: use old per-model storage
   433→      categoryLoras = projectImageSettings.selectedLorasByTextModel[textModel] ?? [];
   434→    }
   435→
   436→    if (categoryLoras.length > 0) {
   437→      loraManager.setSelectedLoras(categoryLoras);
   438→    }
   439→    hasInitializedLoras.current = true;
   440→  }, [projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByCategory, projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByTextModel, projectImageSettings?.generationSource, isLoadingProjectSettings, loraManager, selectedTextModel, generationSource]);
   441→
   442→  // ============================================================================
   443→  // Prompt Management (extracted hook)
   444→  // ============================================================================
   445→  const {
   446→    prompts,
   447→    masterPromptText,
   448→    effectivePromptMode,
   449→    currentBeforePromptText,
   450→    currentAfterPromptText,
   451→    actionablePromptsCount,
   452→    lastKnownPromptCount,
   453→    setPrompts,
   454→    setMasterPromptText,
   455→    setEffectivePromptMode,
   456→    setCurrentBeforePromptText,
   457→    setCurrentAfterPromptText,
   458→    handleAddPrompt,
   459→    handleUpdatePrompt,
   460→    handleRemovePrompt,
   461→    handleDeleteAllPrompts,
   462→    handleSavePromptsFromModal,
   463→  } = usePromptManagement({
   464→    associatedShotId,
   465→    effectiveShotId,
   466→    shotPromptSettings: {
   467→      entityId: shotPromptSettings.entityId,
   468→      status: shotPromptSettings.status,
   469→      settings: shotPromptSettings.settings,
   470→      updateField: shotPromptSettings.updateField,
   471→    },
   472→    noShotPrompts,
   473→    setNoShotPrompts,
   474→    noShotMasterPrompt,
   475→    setNoShotMasterPrompt,
   476→    promptMode,
   477→    setPromptMode,
   478→    beforeEachPromptText,
   479→    setBeforeEachPromptText,
   480→    afterEachPromptText,
   481→    setAfterEachPromptText,
   482→    ready,
   483→    markAsInteracted,
   484→    generatePromptId,
   485→  });
   486→
   487→  // ============================================================================
   488→  // Reference Management (extracted hook)
   489→  // ============================================================================
   490→  const {
   491→    styleReferenceStrength,
   492→    subjectStrength,
   493→    subjectDescription,
   494→    inThisScene,
   495→    inThisSceneStrength,
   496→    referenceMode,
   497→    styleBoostTerms,
   498→    isUploadingStyleReference,
   499→    styleReferenceImageDisplay,
   500→    styleReferenceImageGeneration,
   501→    handleStyleReferenceUpload,
   502→    handleResourceSelect,
   503→    handleSelectReference,
   504→    handleDeleteReference,
   505→    handleUpdateReferenceName,
   506→    handleToggleVisibility,
   507→    handleRemoveStyleReference,
   508→    handleStyleStrengthChange,
   509→    handleSubjectStrengthChange,
   510→    handleSubjectDescriptionChange,
   511→    handleSubjectDescriptionFocus,
   512→    handleSubjectDescriptionBlur,
   513→    handleInThisSceneChange,
   514→    handleInThisSceneStrengthChange,
   515→    handleStyleBoostTermsChange,
   516→    handleReferenceModeChange,
   517→  } = useReferenceManagement({
   518→    selectedProjectId,
   519→    effectiveShotId,
   520→    selectedReferenceId,
   521→    selectedReferenceIdByShot,
   522→    referencePointers,
   523→    hydratedReferences,
   524→    selectedReference,
   525→    isLoadingProjectSettings,
   526→    isLocalGenerationEnabled,
   527→    updateProjectImageSettings,
   528→    markAsInteracted,
   529→    privacyDefaults,
   530→    associatedShotId,
   531→    shotPromptSettings: {
   532→      updateField: shotPromptSettings.updateField,
   533→    },
   534→    setHiresFixConfig,
   535→  });
   536→
   537→  const effectiveSubjectDescription = subjectDescription.trim() || 'this character';
   538→
   539→  // ============================================================================
   540→  // Form Submission (extracted hook)
   541→  // ============================================================================
   542→  const {
   543→    handleSubmit,
   544→    handleGenerateAndQueue,
   545→    handleUseExistingPrompts,
   546→    handleNewPromptsLikeExisting,
   547→  } = useFormSubmission({
   548→    selectedProjectId,
   549→    prompts,
   550→    imagesPerPrompt,
   551→    promptMultiplier,
   552→    associatedShotId,
   553→    currentBeforePromptText,
   554→    currentAfterPromptText,
   555→    styleBoostTerms,
   556→    isLocalGenerationEnabled,
   557→    hiresFixConfig,
   558→    effectivePromptMode,
   559→    masterPromptText,
   560→    actionablePromptsCount,
   561→    generationSourceRef,
   562→    selectedTextModelRef,
   563→    styleReferenceImageGeneration,
   564→    styleReferenceStrength,
   565→    subjectStrength,
   566→    effectiveSubjectDescription,
   567→    inThisScene,
   568→    inThisSceneStrength,
   569→    referenceMode,
   570→    aiGeneratePrompts,
   571→    onGenerate,
   572→    setPrompts,
   573→    automatedSubmitButton,
   574→  });
   575→
   576→

   674→  useEffect(() => {

   685→    const seen = new Set<string>();

   698→

    73→
    74→export function useGenerationSource(props: UseGenerationSourceProps): UseGenerationSourceReturn {
    75→  const {

    77→    projectImageSettings,
    78→    isLoadingProjectSettings,
    79→    updateProjectImageSettings,

    85→
    86→  // ============================================================================
    87→  // State

    89→
    90→  const [generationSource, setGenerationSource] = useState<GenerationSource>('by-reference');
    91→  const [selectedTextModel, setSelectedTextModel] = useState<TextToImageModel>('qwen-image');
    92→  const [modelOverride, setModelOverride] = useState<GenerationMode | undefined>(undefined);
    93→
    94→  // Refs to track current values - prevents stale closure issues in callbacks
    95→  const generationSourceRef = useRef<GenerationSource>(generationSource);

    97→
    98→  useEffect(() => { generationSourceRef.current = generationSource; }, [generationSource]);
    99→  useEffect(() => { selectedTextModelRef.current = selectedTextModel; }, [selectedTextModel]);
   100→

   104→
   105→  // ============================================================================
   106→  // Initialization from Project Settings
   107→  // ============================================================================
   108→
   109→  useEffect(() => {
   110→    if (isLoadingProjectSettings) return;
   111→    if (hasInitializedGenerationSource.current) return;

   113→
   114→    if (projectImageSettings.generationSource) {

   137→    const previousSource = generationSource;

   148→
   149→    // Only swap LORAs if changing categories
   150→    if (previousCategory !== newCategory) {
   151→      const currentLorasByCategory = projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByCategory ?? {
   152→        'qwen': [],
   153→        'z-image': [],
   154→      };
   155→
   156→      // Save current LORAs to the previous category's slot
   157→      const updatedLorasByCategory = {
   158→        ...currentLorasByCategory,
   159→        [previousCategory]: loraManager.selectedLoras,
   160→      };
   161→
   162→      // Load LORAs for the new category
   163→      const newCategoryLoras = currentLorasByCategory[newCategory] ?? [];
   164→      loraManager.setSelectedLoras(newCategoryLoras);
   165→
   166→      try {
   167→        await updateProjectImageSettings('project', {
   168→          generationSource: source,
   169→          selectedLorasByCategory: updatedLorasByCategory,
   170→        });
   171→      } catch (error) {
   172→        handleError(error, { context: 'useGenerationSource.handleGenerationSourceChange', showToast: false });
   173→      }
   174→    } else {
   175→      try {
   176→        await updateProjectImageSettings('project', { generationSource: source });
   177→      } catch (error) {
   178→        handleError(error, { context: 'useGenerationSource.handleGenerationSourceChange', showToast: false });
   179→      }
   180→    }
   181→  }, [updateProjectImageSettings, markAsInteracted, selectedTextModel, generationSource, projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByCategory, loraManager, setHiresFixConfig]);
   182→
   183→  // ============================================================================
   184→  // Handler: Text Model Change
   185→  // ============================================================================
   186→
   187→  const handleTextModelChange = useCallback(async (model: TextToImageModel) => {
   188→    const previousModel = selectedTextModel;
   189→    const previousCategory = getLoraCategoryForModel(previousModel);
   190→    const newCategory = getLoraCategoryForModel(model);
   191→
   192→    setSelectedTextModel(model);
   193→    markAsInteracted();
   194→
   195→    // Apply model-specific hires fix defaults
   196→    setHiresFixConfig(getHiresFixDefaultsForModel(model));
   197→
   198→    // Only swap LORAs if changing categories (qwen ↔ z-image)
   199→    if (previousCategory !== newCategory) {
   200→      const currentLorasByCategory = projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByCategory ?? {
   201→        'qwen': [],
   202→        'z-image': [],
   203→      };
   204→
   205→      // Save current LORAs to the previous category's slot
   206→      const updatedLorasByCategory = {

   213→      loraManager.setSelectedLoras(newCategoryLoras);
   214→
   215→      try {

   223→    } else {

  "new_string": "// ============================================================================\n// Helpers\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n * Build full StyleReferenceMetadata from a HydratedReferenceImage.\n * Centralises the mapping so new metadata fields only need updating in one place.\n */\nfunction buildResourceMetadata(\n  ref: HydratedReferenceImage,\n  overrides: Partial<StyleReferenceMetadata> = {},\n): StyleReferenceMetadata {\n  return {\n    name: ref.name,\n    styleReferenceImage: ref.styleReferenceImage,\n    styleReferenceImageOriginal: ref.styleReferenceImageOriginal,\n    thumbnailUrl: ref.thumbnailUrl,\n    styleReferenceStrength: ref.styleReferenceStrength,\n    subjectStrength: ref.subjectStrength,\n    subjectDescription: ref.subjectDescription,\n    inThisScene: ref.inThisScene,\n    inThisSceneStrength: ref.inThisSceneStrength,\n    referenceMode: ref.referenceMode,\n    styleBoostTerms: ref.styleBoostTerms,\n    created_by: { is_you: true },\n    is_public: ref.isPublic,\n    createdAt: ref.createdAt,\n    updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    ...overrides,\n  };\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Types\n// ============================================================================"
}

Here is a summary of the changes made:

## Changes to `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/ImageGenerationForm/hooks/useReferenceManagement.ts`

### 1. Added `buildResourceMetadata` helper (lines 34-64)

A new helper function was added after the imports and before the types section. It maps a `HydratedReferenceImage` to the full `StyleReferenceMetadata` type, with an optional `overrides` parameter for any fields that differ from the hydrated reference:

```typescript
function buildResourceMetadata(
  ref: HydratedReferenceImage,
  overrides: Partial<StyleReferenceMetadata> = {},
): StyleReferenceMetadata {
  return {
    name: ref.name,
    styleReferenceImage: ref.styleReferenceImage,
    styleReferenceImageOriginal: ref.styleReferenceImageOriginal,
    thumbnailUrl: ref.thumbnailUrl,
    styleReferenceStrength: ref.styleReferenceStrength,
    subjectStrength: ref.subjectStrength,
    subjectDescription: ref.subjectDescription,
    inThisScene: ref.inThisScene,
    inThisSceneStrength: ref.inThisSceneStrength,
    referenceMode: ref.referenceMode,
    styleBoostTerms: ref.styleBoostTerms,
    created_by: { is_you: true },
    is_public: ref.isPublic,
    createdAt: ref.createdAt,
    updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    ...overrides,
  };
}
```

### 2. Simplified `handleUpdateReferenceName` (line 685)

Replaced 16 lines of manual metadata construction with:
```typescript
const metadata = buildResourceMetadata(hydratedRef, { name });
```

### 3. Simplified `handleToggleVisibility` (line 711)

Replaced 16 lines of manual metadata construction with:
```typescript
const metadata = buildResourceMetadata(hydratedRef, { is_public: !currentIsPublic });
```

### Why this matters

Both handlers were manually listing every field of `StyleReferenceMetadata`, which was fragile -- if a new field were added to the type or the `HydratedReferenceImage`, these handlers would silently drop it. The `buildResourceMetadata` helper centralizes the `HydratedReferenceImage` -> `StyleReferenceMetadata` mapping so new fields only need to be added in one place.

`npx tsc --noEmit` passes cleanly with no errors.
