    39→  // LORA manager for category swapping
    40→  loraManager: {
    41→    selectedLoras: ActiveLora[];
    42→    setSelectedLoras: (loras: ActiveLora[]) => void;
    43→  };
    44→  // Callback for hires fix defaults
    45→  setHiresFixConfig: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<HiresFixConfig | Partial<HiresFixConfig>>>;
    46→  // Callback for model change (used by model selector)
    47→  queryClient?: QueryClient;
    48→}
    49→
    50→interface UseGenerationSourceReturn {
    51→  // State
    52→  generationSource: GenerationSource;
    53→  selectedTextModel: TextToImageModel;
    54→  modelOverride: GenerationMode | undefined;
    55→
    56→  // Refs for stale closure prevention
    57→  generationSourceRef: React.MutableRefObject<GenerationSource>;
    58→  selectedTextModelRef: React.MutableRefObject<TextToImageModel>;
    59→
    60→  // Handlers
    61→  handleGenerationSourceChange: (source: GenerationSource) => Promise<void>;
    62→  handleTextModelChange: (model: TextToImageModel) => Promise<void>;
    63→  handleModelChange: (value: GenerationMode) => Promise<void>;
    64→
    65→  // State setters
    66→  setGenerationSource: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<GenerationSource>>;
    67→  setSelectedTextModel: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<TextToImageModel>>;
    68→  setModelOverride: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<GenerationMode | undefined>>;
    69→}
    70→
    71→// ============================================================================
    72→// Hook Implementation
    73→// ============================================================================
    74→
    75→export function useGenerationSource(props: UseGenerationSourceProps): UseGenerationSourceReturn {
    76→  const {
    77→    selectedProjectId,
    78→    projectImageSettings,
    79→    isLoadingProjectSettings,
    80→    updateProjectImageSettings,
    81→    markAsInteracted,
    82→    loraManager,
    83→    setHiresFixConfig,
    84→    queryClient,
    85→  } = props;
    86→
    87→  // ============================================================================
    88→  // State
    89→  // ============================================================================
    90→
    91→  const [generationSource, setGenerationSource] = useState<GenerationSource>('by-reference');
    92→  const [selectedTextModel, setSelectedTextModel] = useState<TextToImageModel>('qwen-image');
    93→  const [modelOverride, setModelOverride] = useState<GenerationMode | undefined>(undefined);
    94→
    95→  // Refs to track current values - prevents stale closure issues in callbacks
    96→  const generationSourceRef = useRef<GenerationSource>(generationSource);
    97→  const selectedTextModelRef = useRef<TextToImageModel>(selectedTextModel);
    98→
    99→  useEffect(() => { generationSourceRef.current = generationSource; }, [generationSource]);
   100→  useEffect(() => { selectedTextModelRef.current = selectedTextModel; }, [selectedTextModel]);
   101→
   102→  // Track initialization
   103→  const hasInitializedGenerationSource = useRef(false);
   104→  const initializedTextModelRef = useRef<TextToImageModel | null>(null);
   105→
   106→  // ============================================================================
   107→  // Initialization from Project Settings
   108→  // ============================================================================
   109→
   110→  useEffect(() => {
   111→    if (isLoadingProjectSettings) return;
   112→    if (hasInitializedGenerationSource.current) return;
   113→    if (!projectImageSettings) return;
   114→
   115→    if (projectImageSettings.generationSource) {
   116→      setGenerationSource(projectImageSettings.generationSource);
   117→    }
   118→    const textModel = projectImageSettings.selectedTextModel || 'qwen-image';
   119→    if (projectImageSettings.selectedTextModel) {
   120→      setSelectedTextModel(projectImageSettings.selectedTextModel);
   121→    }
   122→    initializedTextModelRef.current = textModel;
   123→    hasInitializedGenerationSource.current = true;
   124→  }, [projectImageSettings, isLoadingProjectSettings]);
   125→
   126→  // Initialize LORAs from per-category storage (runs after generation source init)
   127→  // Categories: 'qwen' (all Qwen models + by-reference) and 'z-image'
   128→  const hasInitializedLoras = useRef(false);
   129→  useEffect(() => {
   130→    if (isLoadingProjectSettings) return;
   131→    if (hasInitializedLoras.current) return;
   132→
   133→    // Determine current category based on generation source and model
   134→    const textModel = initializedTextModelRef.current || selectedTextModel;
   135→    const currentSource = projectImageSettings?.generationSource || generationSource;
   136→    // by-reference always uses 'qwen' category
   137→    const category: LoraCategory = currentSource === 'by-reference' ? 'qwen' : getLoraCategoryForModel(textModel);
   138→
   139→    // Try new category-based storage first, fall back to old per-model storage for migration
   140→    let categoryLoras: ActiveLora[] = [];
   141→    if (projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByCategory) {
   142→      categoryLoras = projectImageSettings.selectedLorasByCategory[category] ?? [];
   143→    } else if (projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByTextModel) {
   144→      // Migration: use old per-model storage
   145→      categoryLoras = projectImageSettings.selectedLorasByTextModel[textModel] ?? [];
   146→    }
   147→
   148→    if (categoryLoras.length > 0) {
   149→      loraManager.setSelectedLoras(categoryLoras);
   150→    }
   151→    hasInitializedLoras.current = true;
   152→  }, [projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByCategory, projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByTextModel, projectImageSettings?.generationSource, isLoadingProjectSettings, loraManager, selectedTextModel, generationSource]);
   153→
   154→  // Clear model override once server settings reflect the change
   155→  useEffect(() => {
   156→    if (modelOverride && projectImageSettings?.selectedModel === modelOverride) {
   157→      setModelOverride(undefined);
   158→    }
   159→  }, [projectImageSettings?.selectedModel, modelOverride]);
   160→
   161→  // ============================================================================
   162→  // Handler: Generation Source Change
   163→  // ============================================================================
   164→
   165→  const handleGenerationSourceChange = useCallback(async (source: GenerationSource) => {
   166→    const previousSource = generationSource;
   167→    setGenerationSource(source);
   168→    markAsInteracted();
   169→
   170→    // Apply model-specific hires fix defaults when switching modes
   171→    const modelName = source === 'by-reference' ? 'qwen-image' : selectedTextModel;
   172→    setHiresFixConfig(getHiresFixDefaultsForModel(modelName));
   173→
   174→    // Determine categories for LORA swapping
   175→    const previousCategory: LoraCategory = previousSource === 'by-reference' ? 'qwen' : getLoraCategoryForModel(selectedTextModel);
   176→    const newCategory: LoraCategory = source === 'by-reference' ? 'qwen' : getLoraCategoryForModel(selectedTextModel);
   177→
   178→    // Only swap LORAs if changing categories
   179→    if (previousCategory !== newCategory) {
   180→      const currentLorasByCategory = projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByCategory ?? {
   181→        'qwen': [],
   182→        'z-image': [],
   183→      };
   184→
   185→      // Save current LORAs to the previous category's slot
   186→      const updatedLorasByCategory = {
   187→        ...currentLorasByCategory,
   188→        [previousCategory]: loraManager.selectedLoras,
   189→      };
   190→
   191→      // Load LORAs for the new category
   192→      const newCategoryLoras = currentLorasByCategory[newCategory] ?? [];
   193→      loraManager.setSelectedLoras(newCategoryLoras);
   194→
   195→      try {
   196→        await updateProjectImageSettings('project', {
   197→          generationSource: source,
   198→          selectedLorasByCategory: updatedLorasByCategory,
   199→        });
   200→      } catch (error) {
   201→        handleError(error, { context: 'useGenerationSource.handleGenerationSourceChange', showToast: false });
   202→      }
   203→    } else {
   204→      try {
   205→        await updateProjectImageSettings('project', { generationSource: source });
   206→      } catch (error) {
   207→        handleError(error, { context: 'useGenerationSource.handleGenerationSourceChange', showToast: false });
   208→      }
   209→    }
   210→  }, [updateProjectImageSettings, markAsInteracted, selectedTextModel, generationSource, projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByCategory, loraManager, setHiresFixConfig]);
   211→
   212→  // ============================================================================
   213→  // Handler: Text Model Change
   214→  // ============================================================================
   215→
   216→  const handleTextModelChange = useCallback(async (model: TextToImageModel) => {
   217→    const previousModel = selectedTextModel;
   218→    const previousCategory = getLoraCategoryForModel(previousModel);
   219→    const newCategory = getLoraCategoryForModel(model);
   220→
   221→    setSelectedTextModel(model);
   222→    markAsInteracted();
   223→
   224→    // Apply model-specific hires fix defaults
   225→    setHiresFixConfig(getHiresFixDefaultsForModel(model));
   226→
   227→    // Only swap LORAs if changing categories (qwen ↔ z-image)
   228→    if (previousCategory !== newCategory) {
   229→      const currentLorasByCategory = projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByCategory ?? {
   230→        'qwen': [],
   231→        'z-image': [],
   232→      };
   233→
   234→      // Save current LORAs to the previous category's slot
   235→      const updatedLorasByCategory = {
   236→        ...currentLorasByCategory,
   237→        [previousCategory]: loraManager.selectedLoras,
   238→      };
   239→
   240→      // Load LORAs for the new category
   241→      const newCategoryLoras = currentLorasByCategory[newCategory] ?? [];
   242→      loraManager.setSelectedLoras(newCategoryLoras);
   243→
   244→      try {
   245→        await updateProjectImageSettings('project', {
   246→          selectedTextModel: model,
   247→          selectedLorasByCategory: updatedLorasByCategory,
   248→        });
   249→      } catch (error) {
   250→        handleError(error, { context: 'useGenerationSource.handleTextModelChange', showToast: false });
   251→      }
   252→    } else {
   253→      try {
   254→        await updateProjectImageSettings('project', { selectedTextModel: model });
   255→      } catch (error) {
   256→        handleError(error, { context: 'useGenerationSource.handleTextModelChange', showToast: false });
   257→      }
   258→    }
   259→  }, [updateProjectImageSettings, markAsInteracted, selectedTextModel, projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByCategory, loraManager, setHiresFixConfig, generationSource]);
   260→
   261→  // ============================================================================
   262→  // Handler: Model Change (Legacy)
   263→  // ============================================================================
   264→
   265→  const handleModelChange = useCallback(async (value: GenerationMode) => {
   266→
   267→    // Optimistic UI flip
   268→    setModelOverride(value);
   269→
   270→    // Optimistically update settings cache
   271→    if (queryClient) {
   272→      try {
   273→        queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.settings.tool('project-image-settings', selectedProjectId, undefined), (prev: unknown) =>
   274→          updateSettingsCache<ProjectImageSettings>(prev, { selectedModel: value })
   275→        );
   276→      } catch (e) {
   277→      }
   278→    }
   279→
   280→    // Clear LoRAs when switching to Qwen.Image
   281→    if (value === 'qwen-image') {
   282→      loraManager.setSelectedLoras([]);
   283→    }
   284→
   285→    await updateProjectImageSettings('project', { selectedModel: value });
   286→    markAsInteracted();
   287→  }, [queryClient, selectedProjectId, loraManager, updateProjectImageSettings, markAsInteracted]);
   288→
   289→  // ============================================================================
   290→  // Return
   291→  // ============================================================================
   292→
   293→  return {
   294→    // State
   295→    generationSource,
   296→    selectedTextModel,
   297→    modelOverride,
   298→
   299→    // Refs
   300→    generationSourceRef,
   301→    selectedTextModelRef,
   302→
   303→    // Handlers
   304→    handleGenerationSourceChange,
   305→    handleTextModelChange,
   306→    handleModelChange,
   307→
   308→    // State setters
   309→    setGenerationSource,
   310→    setSelectedTextModel,
   311→    setModelOverride,
   312→  };
   313→}
